===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\DEPLOYMENT.md =====
# QA Management Platform - Production Deployment Guide

This guide outlines the steps to deploy the QA Management Platform to production on Vercel and Supabase.

## Architecture Overview
- **Frontend & Server Actions**: Next.js 14 App Router deployed on Vercel.
- **Database & Auth**: Supabase PostgreSQL with Row Level Security (RLS).
- **Styling**: Tailwind CSS with custom dark navy/neutral design system.

---

## Prerequisites
1. [Supabase Account](https://supabase.com)
2. [Vercel Account](https://vercel.com)
3. [GitHub Repository](https://github.com)

---

## Step 1: Database Setup on Supabase

1. Create a new project in Supabase.
2. Navigate to the **SQL Editor** in Supabase Studio.
3. Copy and execute the complete schema migration script from `supabase_schema.sql` (found in the root outputs directory).
4. For an existing database, or after applying the main schema, execute `supabase_onboarding_migration.sql` in the SQL Editor. It backfills Auth users into `profiles`, syncs future profile/email changes, and adds per-user profile policies.
5. Verify that RLS policies are active on all tables.

---

## Step 2: Environment Variables Configuration

Set up the following environment variables in Vercel project settings:

```env
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL=https://your-project.supabase.co
NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY=your-supabase-anon-key
SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY=your-supabase-secret-key
NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL=https://your-domain.com
```

---

## Step 3: Deploy to Vercel

1. Push your repository to GitHub.
2. Import the repository into Vercel.
3. Configure project settings:
   - Framework Preset: **Next.js**
   - Root Directory: `./` (or your subfolder)
   - Build Command: `npm run build`
4. Add the environment variables from Step 2.
5. Click **Deploy**.

---

## Step 4: Verification & Smoke Testing

Once deployed, verify the following core user journeys:
1. **Authentication**: Register a new user account, log in, and verify password recovery flow.
2. **Account management**: Open the account menu to update a profile or sign out. Email changes may require confirmation.
3. **Organization Multi-Tenancy**: Create a new organization; the creating user is added as its admin. Then invite team members with specific roles (`org_admin`, `qa_manager`, `qa_engineer`, `developer`, `viewer`).
4. **Project & Requirements**: Create a new project and add requirements with priorities and statuses.
5. **Test Cases & Execution**: Create test cases with step-by-step actions, build test plans, and execute test runs.
6. **Defect Tracking**: Report defects, assign to developers, and link them to test executions.
7. **Traceability & Reports**: Review the requirements traceability matrix and export summary reports.
8. **AI Assistant**: Test the AI test case generator with human approval workflow.

---

## Security Best Practices
- Keep `SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY` server-only; never expose it to the frontend or commit it to source control.
- Regularly audit PostgreSQL RLS policies in Supabase.
- Enforce strict role-based access control (RBAC) across all server actions.

# Milestone 2: Project Setup, UI Foundation & Authentication

## Task Overview
Milestone 2 constructs the development foundation: project scaffolding, TypeScript type safety, UI component libraries, authentication flows, and responsive dashboard skeleton.

## Part 1: Scaffold Project with Manual Setup

Due to offline registry constraints, initialize a new Next.js application via `npm init` and manually configure required files. Once network is available, production setup will use `npx create-next-app` with flags below:

```
npx create-next-app@latest qa-platform \
  --typescript \
  --tailwind \
  --app \
  --no-src-dir \
  --import-alias "@/*" \
  --use-npm \
  --yes
```

For offline progress, we create initial project structure with:

```
qa-platform/
+-- package.json
+-- tsconfig.json
+-- next.config.mjs
+-- tailwind.config.ts
+-- postcss.config.js
+-- .eslintrc.json
+-- src/
¦   +-- app/
¦   ¦   +-- (auth)/
¦   ¦   +-- (dashboard)/
¦   ¦   +-- layout.tsx
¦   ¦   +-- page.tsx
¦   +-- components/
¦   ¦   +-- ui/
¦   ¦   +-- common/
¦   +-- lib/
¦   ¦   +-- supabase/
¦   +-- types/
```

## Part 2: Configuration Files

**tsconfig.json** (TypeScript path aliases, strict mode)
```json
{
  "compilerOptions": {
    "target": "ES2022",
    "lib": ["dom", "dom.iterable", "esnext"],
    "allowJs": true,
    "skipLibCheck": true,
    "strict": true,
    "noEmit": true,
    "esModuleInterop": true,
    "module": "esnext",
    "moduleResolution": "bundler",
    "resolveJsonModule": true,
    "isolatedModules": true,
    "jsx": "preserve",
    "incremental": true,
    "paths": {
      "@/*": ["./src/*"]
    }
  },
  "include": ["next-env.d.ts", "**/*.ts", "**/*.tsx", ".next/types/**/*.ts"],
  "exclude": ["node_modules"]
}
```

**tailwind.config.ts** (Dark navy and neutral palette per design brief)
```ts
import type { Config } from "tailwindcss";

const config: Config = {
  content: [
    "./src/pages/**/*.{js,ts,jsx,tsx,mdx}",
    "./src/components/**/*.{js,ts,jsx,tsx,mdx}",
    "./src/app/**/*.{js,ts,jsx,tsx,mdx}",
  ],
  theme: {
    extend: {
      colors: {
        background: "#0A0F1C",
        foreground: "#E8ECF4",
        primary: {
          DEFAULT: "#1B3A6B",
          foreground: "#FFFFFF",
        },
        secondary: {
          DEFAULT: "#2D3748",
          foreground: "#E8ECF4",
        },
        muted: {
          DEFAULT: "#1E293B",
          foreground: "#8B9BB8",
        }
      }
    },
  },
  plugins: [],
};
export default config;
```

**postcss.config.js**
```js
module.exports = {
  plugins: {
    tailwindcss: {},
    autoprefixer: {},
  },
}
```

## Part 3: Supabase Authentication Integration

### Supabase Client Setup
File: `src/lib/supabase/client.ts`

Initialize Supabase client for both server and client contexts. Uses environment variables for URL and anon key—no service role exposure.

## Part 4: Supabase Auth UX Flows

- **Login**: Email/password login screen (`/auth/login`) with form validation (zod).
- **Register / Onboarding**: `/auth/register` ? create user profile ? prompt to create/join an organization ? redirect to dashboard.
- **Forgot Password**: `/auth/reset-password` flow with email verification & token validation.
- **Session Management**: Next.js middleware enforces auth redirects for protected `(dashboard)` routes.

## Part 5: Core UI Component Foundation

Install shadcn/ui primitives for:
- Buttons, Inputs, Forms, Dialogs, Dropdown Menus, Tooltips
- Tables with sorting & filtering
- Sidebars, Navbars, Breadcrumbs
- Cards for dashboard metrics

## Part 6: Dashboard Skeleton Structure

Create responsive shell:
- App-wide Header: organization switcher, global search placeholder, user profile menu, notifications
- Sidebar Navigation: Dashboard, Projects, Requirements, Test Cases, Test Plans, Test Execution, Defects, Traceability, Reports, AI Assistant, Settings
- Layout with desktop and tablet breakpoints per design brief.

## Part 7: Database Seed Initial Dataset

Use `supabase_schema.sql` for full schema. Seed with:

- One organization: `Acme QA Labs`
- Profiles for test roles: QA Manager, QA Engineer, Developer
- Projects: `Mobile Banking App`, `E-Commerce Platform`
- Seed requirements, test cases, and a test plan for MVP validation.

## Part 8: Development Scripts

- `npm run dev` ? Next.js dev server (Turbopack)
- `npm run build` ? production build
- `npm run lint` ? ESLint
- `npm run type-check` ? TypeScript compiler

## Acceptance Criteria

1. Clean `npm start` launches Next.js dev server locally without errors.
2. Supabase auth flows (login, register, reset password) complete successfully.
3. Protected routes redirect unauthenticated users to `/auth/login`.
4. UI foundation renders with navy/neutral palette, responsive sidebar + header.
5. Supabase schema is deployed to dev project and RLS policies are active.
6. Seed data visible in Supabase Studio (organizations, projects, requirements, test cases).

## Next Steps
Milestone 2 establishes foundation for Milestone 3 (Multi-Tenant Organization Management & RBAC).

# Milestone 3: Multi-Tenant Organization Management & RBAC

## Overview
Milestone 3 implements complete multi-tenant architecture with strict data isolation and role-based access control.

## Deliverables

### 1. Organization Management
- Organization creation flow with slug validation
- Organization profile management
- Organization switching interface
- Organization settings page

### 2. Team Member Management
- Member invitation system
- Role assignment (super_admin, org_admin, qa_manager, qa_engineer, developer, viewer)
- Member removal and role changes
- Activity tracking for members

### 3. Row Level Security (RLS) Implementation
- PostgreSQL RLS policies on all tables
- Organization isolation at database level
- Cascade policies for related tables
- Security definer functions for helper queries

### 4. Backend API Routes
- `/api/organizations` - CRUD operations
- `/api/organizations/[id]/members` - Member management
- `/api/organizations/[id]/projects` - Project listing with isolation

### 5. RBAC Middleware
- Session-based permission checks
- Permission hooks for components
- Route guards for protected pages
- Organization context provider

## Database Changes
- organization_members table with role enum
- RLS policies on all tables
- Helper functions for user organization access

## Security Controls
- Database-level tenant isolation via RLS
- Backend permission validation
- Frontend UI visibility based on roles
- Audit logging for sensitive operations

## Acceptance Criteria
1. Users can create organizations
2. Users can switch between organizations
3. Role permissions enforced at database level
4. Members can only see data from their organizations
5. Admin roles can manage members
6. All API routes enforce RLS

# QA Management Platform

Modern SaaS platform for software testing lifecycle management.

## Technology Stack

- **Frontend**: Next.js 14 (App Router), TypeScript, Tailwind CSS
- **Backend**: Next.js Server Actions, Supabase
- **Database**: PostgreSQL with Row Level Security (RLS)
- **Authentication**: Supabase Auth

## Getting Started

1. Install dependencies:
```bash
npm install
```

2. Set up environment variables:
```bash
cp .env.example .env.local
```

3. Run development server:
```bash
npm run dev
```

## Features

- Multi-tenant organization management
- Project and requirements tracking
- Test case management
- Test execution engine
- Defect tracking
- Traceability matrix
- AI-powered QA assistant

## Milestones

- Milestone 1: Architecture & MVP Planning ?
- Milestone 2: Project Setup, UI & Auth Foundation
- Milestone 3: Multi-Tenancy & RBAC
- Milestone 4: Project & Requirements Management
- Milestone 5: Test Case Management
- Milestone 6: Test Plans & Execution
- Milestone 7: Defect Management
- Milestone 8: Traceability & Reporting
- Milestone 9: AI QA Assistant
- Milestone 10: Testing & Production Prep



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\supabase_onboarding_migration.sql =====
-- Run this once in the Supabase SQL Editor after deploying the profile/onboarding changes.

-- Backfill profiles for Auth users created before profile synchronization was installed.
INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, email, full_name)
SELECT
  users.id,
  users.email,
  COALESCE(
    NULLIF(BTRIM(COALESCE(users.raw_user_meta_data ->> 'full_name', users.raw_user_meta_data ->> 'name')), ''),
    NULLIF(SPLIT_PART(users.email, '@', 1), ''),
    'QA User'
  )
FROM auth.users AS users
WHERE users.email IS NOT NULL
ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE
SET
  email = EXCLUDED.email,
  full_name = CASE
    WHEN NULLIF(BTRIM(public.profiles.full_name), '') IS NULL THEN EXCLUDED.full_name
    ELSE public.profiles.full_name
  END;

-- Keep the application profile in sync with Auth sign-ups and profile/email changes.
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.sync_profile_from_auth_user()
RETURNS trigger
LANGUAGE plpgsql
SECURITY DEFINER
SET search_path = public
AS $$
DECLARE
  profile_email text;
  profile_name text;
  auth_name text;
BEGIN
  profile_email := NULLIF(BTRIM(NEW.email), '');
  IF profile_email IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NEW;
  END IF;

  auth_name := NULLIF(BTRIM(COALESCE(NEW.raw_user_meta_data ->> 'full_name', NEW.raw_user_meta_data ->> 'name')), '');
  profile_name := COALESCE(auth_name, NULLIF(SPLIT_PART(profile_email, '@', 1), ''), 'QA User');

  INSERT INTO public.profiles (id, email, full_name)
  VALUES (NEW.id, profile_email, profile_name)
  ON CONFLICT (id) DO UPDATE
  SET
    email = EXCLUDED.email,
    full_name = CASE
      WHEN auth_name IS NOT NULL THEN EXCLUDED.full_name
      WHEN NULLIF(BTRIM(public.profiles.full_name), '') IS NULL THEN EXCLUDED.full_name
      ELSE public.profiles.full_name
    END;

  RETURN NEW;
END;
$$;

DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS sync_profile_from_auth_user ON auth.users;
CREATE TRIGGER sync_profile_from_auth_user
AFTER INSERT OR UPDATE ON auth.users
FOR EACH ROW
EXECUTE FUNCTION public.sync_profile_from_auth_user();

-- Users can read and edit only their own profile. New profile rows are created by the Auth trigger.
ALTER TABLE public.profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
DROP POLICY IF EXISTS profiles_read_self ON public.profiles;
CREATE POLICY profiles_read_self ON public.profiles
  FOR SELECT USING (id = auth.uid());

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS profiles_update_self ON public.profiles;
CREATE POLICY profiles_update_self ON public.profiles
  FOR UPDATE USING (id = auth.uid())
  WITH CHECK (id = auth.uid());




===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\supabase_schema.sql =====
-- ============================================================================
-- QA MANAGEMENT PLATFORM - SUPABASE POSTGRESQL MULTI-TENANT SCHEMA & RLS
-- ============================================================================

-- 1. Enable UUID Extension
CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS "uuid-ossp";

-- 2. Organizations Table (Tenants)
CREATE TABLE organizations (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    slug VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    plan VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'enterprise',
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 3. Profiles Table (User Metadata extending auth.users)
CREATE TABLE profiles (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    email VARCHAR(255) UNIQUE NOT NULL,
    full_name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    avatar_url TEXT,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 4. Organization Members (Tenant Membership & RBAC Roles)
CREATE TYPE user_role AS ENUM (
    'super_admin',
    'org_admin',
    'qa_manager',
    'qa_engineer',
    'developer',
    'viewer'
);

CREATE TABLE organization_members (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    user_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES profiles(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    role user_role NOT NULL DEFAULT 'qa_engineer',
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE(organization_id, user_id)
);

-- Helper function to get current user's organization IDs
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_user_organization_ids(user_uuid UUID)
RETURNS SETOF UUID AS $$
BEGIN
    RETURN QUERY
    SELECT organization_id FROM organization_members WHERE user_id = user_uuid;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- 5. Projects Table
CREATE TABLE projects (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    key VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'active',
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE(organization_id, key)
);

-- 6. Releases & Versions
CREATE TABLE releases (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    version_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    start_date DATE,
    release_date DATE,
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'planning',
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

-- 7. Requirements Management
CREATE TYPE req_priority AS ENUM ('low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical');
CREATE TYPE req_status AS ENUM ('draft', 'under_review', 'approved', 'implemented', 'verified', 'rejected');
CREATE TYPE req_type AS ENUM ('functional', 'non_functional', 'business', 'performance', 'security');

CREATE TABLE requirements (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    req_key VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    priority req_priority DEFAULT 'medium',
    status req_status DEFAULT 'draft',
    type req_type DEFAULT 'functional',
    version INT DEFAULT 1,
    owner_id UUID REFERENCES profiles(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE(project_id, req_key)
);

-- 8. Test Cases Management
CREATE TYPE test_priority AS ENUM ('low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical');
CREATE TYPE test_type AS ENUM ('functional', 'regression', 'integration', 'system', 'api', 'ui', 'performance', 'security', 'exploratory', 'uat');
CREATE TYPE automation_status AS ENUM ('manual', 'automated', 'to_automate', 'not_automatable');

CREATE TABLE test_cases (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    case_key VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    preconditions TEXT,
    priority test_priority DEFAULT 'medium',
    test_type test_type DEFAULT 'functional',
    category VARCHAR(100) DEFAULT 'General',
    automation_status automation_status DEFAULT 'manual',
    tags TEXT[],
    author_id UUID REFERENCES profiles(id),
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE(project_id, case_key)
);

CREATE TABLE test_case_steps (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    test_case_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_cases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    step_number INT NOT NULL,
    action TEXT NOT NULL,
    expected_result TEXT NOT NULL
);

CREATE TABLE test_case_requirements (
    test_case_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_cases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    requirement_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES requirements(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    PRIMARY KEY (test_case_id, requirement_id)
);

-- 9. Test Plans & Suites
CREATE TABLE test_plans (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    title VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    scope TEXT,
    release_id UUID REFERENCES releases(id),
    start_date DATE,
    end_date DATE,
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'draft',
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP
);

CREATE TABLE test_suites (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    test_plan_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_plans(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT
);

CREATE TABLE test_suite_cases (
    test_suite_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_suites(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    test_case_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_cases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    PRIMARY KEY (test_suite_id, test_case_id)
);

-- 10. Test Execution Engine
CREATE TABLE test_runs (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    test_plan_id UUID REFERENCES test_plans(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    name VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    assigned_to UUID REFERENCES profiles(id),
    status VARCHAR(50) DEFAULT 'in_progress',
    started_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    completed_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
);

CREATE TYPE execution_status AS ENUM ('not_run', 'passed', 'failed', 'blocked', 'not_applicable');

CREATE TABLE test_executions (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    test_run_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_runs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    test_case_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_cases(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    tester_id UUID REFERENCES profiles(id),
    status execution_status DEFAULT 'not_run',
    actual_result TEXT,
    comments TEXT,
    executed_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE
);

CREATE TABLE execution_steps (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    test_execution_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES test_executions(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    step_number INT NOT NULL,
    status execution_status DEFAULT 'not_run',
    actual_result TEXT
);

-- 11. Defect Management
CREATE TYPE defect_severity AS ENUM ('trivial', 'minor', 'major', 'critical', 'blocker');
CREATE TYPE defect_status AS ENUM ('new', 'open', 'in_progress', 'fixed', 'ready_for_retest', 'reopened', 'closed', 'rejected');

CREATE TABLE defects (
    id UUID PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT uuid_generate_v4(),
    project_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES projects(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    organization_id UUID NOT NULL REFERENCES organizations(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    defect_key VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    summary VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    severity defect_severity DEFAULT 'major',
    priority test_priority DEFAULT 'medium',
    status defect_status DEFAULT 'new',
    environment VARCHAR(100),
    steps_to_reproduce TEXT,
    expected_result TEXT,
    actual_result TEXT,
    assigned_to UUID REFERENCES profiles(id),
    reporter_id UUID REFERENCES profiles(id),
    test_case_id UUID REFERENCES test_cases(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    test_execution_id UUID REFERENCES test_executions(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    created_at TIMESTAMP WITH TIME ZONE DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,
    UNIQUE(project_id, defect_key)
);

-- ============================================================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY (RLS) POLICIES
-- ============================================================================

ALTER TABLE organizations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE organization_members ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE projects ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE releases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE requirements ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_cases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_case_steps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_case_requirements ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_plans ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_suites ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_suite_cases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_runs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_executions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE execution_steps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE defects ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Organizations Policy
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_policy ON organizations
    FOR ALL USING (id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Organization Members Policy
CREATE POLICY org_members_isolation ON organization_members
    FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Projects Policy
CREATE POLICY projects_isolation ON projects
    FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Requirements Policy
CREATE POLICY requirements_isolation ON requirements
    FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Test Cases Policy
CREATE POLICY test_cases_isolation ON test_cases
    FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Test Case Requirements Policy - both linked records must be visible to the user.
CREATE POLICY test_case_requirements_isolation ON test_case_requirements
    FOR ALL USING (
        EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM requirements r
            JOIN test_cases tc ON tc.project_id = r.project_id
            WHERE r.id = test_case_requirements.requirement_id
              AND tc.id = test_case_requirements.test_case_id
        )
    )
    WITH CHECK (
        EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM requirements r
            JOIN test_cases tc ON tc.project_id = r.project_id
            WHERE r.id = test_case_requirements.requirement_id
              AND tc.id = test_case_requirements.test_case_id
        )
    );

-- Test Plans Policy
CREATE POLICY test_plans_isolation ON test_plans
    FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Test Runs Policy
CREATE POLICY test_runs_isolation ON test_runs
    FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Test Executions Policy
CREATE POLICY test_executions_isolation ON test_executions
    FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Defects Policy
CREATE POLICY defects_isolation ON defects
    FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

DROP POLICY IF EXISTS test_case_requirements_isolation ON public.test_case_requirements;
CREATE POLICY test_case_requirements_isolation ON public.test_case_requirements
  FOR ALL USING (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM public.requirements r
      JOIN public.test_cases tc ON tc.project_id = r.project_id
      WHERE r.id = public.test_case_requirements.requirement_id
        AND tc.id = public.test_case_requirements.test_case_id
    )
  )
  WITH CHECK (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM public.requirements r
      JOIN public.test_cases tc ON tc.project_id = r.project_id
      WHERE r.id = public.test_case_requirements.requirement_id
        AND tc.id = public.test_case_requirements.test_case_id

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\middleware.ts =====
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from 'next/server';

export async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {
  let response = NextResponse.next({
    request: {
      headers: request.headers,
    },
  });

  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return request.cookies.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          request.cookies.set({ name, value, ...options });
          response = NextResponse.next({
            request: {
              headers: request.headers,
            },
          });
          response.cookies.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          request.cookies.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
          response = NextResponse.next({
            request: {
              headers: request.headers,
            },
          });
          response.cookies.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;
  const protectedRoutes = [
    '/dashboard', '/projects', '/organizations', '/profile', '/settings',
    '/requirements', '/test-cases', '/test-plans', '/test-runs', '/defects', '/reports',
  ];
  const authRoutes = ['/auth/login', '/auth/register', '/auth/forgot-password'];

  const isProtected = protectedRoutes.some(route => pathname.startsWith(route));
  const isAuth = authRoutes.some(route => pathname.startsWith(route));

  // If not logged in and accessing protected route -> redirect to login
  if (isProtected && !user) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/auth/login', request.url));
  }

  // If already logged in and visiting login/register -> redirect to dashboard
  if (isAuth && user) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL('/dashboard', request.url));
  }

  return response;
}

export const config = {
  matcher: ['/((?!api|_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico).*)'],
};

import { AuthProvider } from "@/components/auth/AuthProvider";
import { ThemeProvider } from "@/components/theme/ThemeProvider";
import "./globals.css";

export const metadata: Metadata = {
  title: "QA Management Platform",
  description: "Modern SaaS platform for software testing lifecycle management",
};

export default function RootLayout({
  children,
}: {
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <html lang="en" suppressHydrationWarning>
      <head>
        <script
          dangerouslySetInnerHTML={{
            __html: `
              try {
                var theme = localStorage.getItem('qa_theme');
                if (theme === 'dark' || (!theme && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches)) {
                  document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
                } else {
                  document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
                }
              } catch (_) {}
            `,
          }}
        />
      </head>
      <body className="font-sans antialiased bg-background text-foreground transition-colors duration-150">
        <ThemeProvider>
          <AuthProvider>{children}</AuthProvider>
        </ThemeProvider>
      </body>
    </html>
  );
}

   - Expected Result: Error message 'Invalid credentials' is displayed, user remains on login page

              {output && (
                <div className="mt-4 flex gap-3">
                  <button
                    type="button"

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\api\dashboard\route.ts =====
import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';

export async function GET() {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { data: memberships, error: membershipError } = await supabase
    .from('organization_members')
    .select('organization_id')
    .eq('user_id', user.id);

  if (membershipError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load organization access.' }, { status: 500 });
  }

  const organizationIds = memberships?.map((membership) => membership.organization_id) ?? [];
  if (organizationIds.length === 0) {
    return NextResponse.json({
      stats: {
        projectCount: 0,
        requirementCount: 0,
        testCaseCount: 0,
        passRate: null,
        openDefectCount: 0,
        criticalDefectCount: 0,
      },
      executionProgress: [],
      recentDefects: [],
    });
  }

  const [
    projects,
    requirements,
    testCases,
    openDefects,
    criticalDefects,
    passedExecutions,
    failedExecutions,
    recentDefects,
    recentRuns,
  ] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from('projects').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).in('organization_id', organizationIds),
    supabase.from('requirements').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).in('organization_id', organizationIds),
    supabase.from('test_cases').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).in('organization_id', organizationIds),
    supabase.from('defects').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).in('organization_id', organizationIds)
      .in('status', ['new', 'open', 'in_progress', 'ready_for_retest', 'reopened']),
    supabase.from('defects').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true }).in('organization_id', organizationIds)
      .in('severity', ['critical', 'blocker']).not('status', 'in', '(closed,rejected)'),
    supabase.from('test_executions').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .in('organization_id', organizationIds).eq('status', 'passed'),
    supabase.from('test_executions').select('id', { count: 'exact', head: true })
      .in('organization_id', organizationIds).eq('status', 'failed'),
    supabase.from('defects').select('id, defect_key, summary, severity, status')
      .in('organization_id', organizationIds).order('created_at', { ascending: false }).limit(3),
    supabase.from('test_runs').select('id, name')
      .in('organization_id', organizationIds).order('started_at', { ascending: false }).limit(3),
  ]);

  const errors = [projects.error, requirements.error, testCases.error, openDefects.error,
    criticalDefects.error, passedExecutions.error, failedExecutions.error, recentDefects.error, recentRuns.error];
  if (errors.some(Boolean)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load dashboard data.' }, { status: 500 });
  }

  const executionProgress = await Promise.all((recentRuns.data ?? []).map(async (run) => {
    const { data: executions, error } = await supabase
      .from('test_executions')
      .select('status')
      .eq('test_run_id', run.id);

    if (error) return { id: run.id, name: run.name, progress: null, total: 0 };
    const total = executions?.length ?? 0;
    const completed = executions?.filter((execution) => execution.status !== 'not_run').length ?? 0;
    return {
      id: run.id,
      name: run.name,
      progress: total > 0 ? Math.round((completed / total) * 100) : 0,
      total,
    };
  }));

  const passed = passedExecutions.count ?? 0;
  const failed = failedExecutions.count ?? 0;
  const completedExecutionCount = passed + failed;

  return NextResponse.json({
    stats: {
      projectCount: projects.count ?? 0,
      requirementCount: requirements.count ?? 0,
      testCaseCount: testCases.count ?? 0,
      passRate: completedExecutionCount > 0 ? Math.round((passed / completedExecutionCount) * 100) : null,
      openDefectCount: openDefects.count ?? 0,
      criticalDefectCount: criticalDefects.count ?? 0,
    },
    executionProgress,
    recentDefects: recentDefects.data ?? [],
  });
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\api\defects\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createRouteSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/route-client';

const severities = ['trivial', 'minor', 'major', 'critical', 'blocker'] as const;
const priorities = ['low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical'] as const;

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRouteSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const projectId = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get('projectId');
  let query = supabase.from('defects').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
  if (projectId) query = query.eq('project_id', projectId);
  const { data, error } = await query;
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load defects.' }, { status: 500 });
  return NextResponse.json({ defects: data ?? [] });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRouteSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  let body: Record<string, unknown>;
  try { body = await request.json(); } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Request body must be valid JSON.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const projectId = typeof body.projectId === 'string' ? body.projectId : '';
  const defectKey = typeof body.defectKey === 'string' ? body.defectKey.trim().toUpperCase() : '';
  const summary = typeof body.summary === 'string' ? body.summary.trim() : '';
  if (!projectId || !defectKey || !summary) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Project, defect key, and summary are required.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const { data: project, error: projectError } = await supabase
    .from('projects').select('id, organization_id').eq('id', projectId).maybeSingle();
  if (projectError || !project) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Select a project you can access.' }, { status: 400 });

  const severity = typeof body.severity === 'string' && severities.includes(body.severity as typeof severities[number])
    ? body.severity : 'major';
  const priority = typeof body.priority === 'string' && priorities.includes(body.priority as typeof priorities[number])
    ? body.priority : 'medium';

  const { data: defect, error } = await supabase.from('defects').insert({
    project_id: project.id,
    organization_id: project.organization_id,
    defect_key: defectKey,
    summary,
    description: typeof body.description === 'string' ? body.description.trim() || null : null,
    severity,
    priority,
    status: 'new',
    environment: typeof body.environment === 'string' ? body.environment.trim() || null : null,
    steps_to_reproduce: typeof body.stepsToReproduce === 'string' ? body.stepsToReproduce.trim() || null : null,
    expected_result: typeof body.expectedResult === 'string' ? body.expectedResult.trim() || null : null,
    actual_result: typeof body.actualResult === 'string' ? body.actualResult.trim() || null : null,
    reporter_id: user.id,
  }).select().single();

  if (error) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error.code === '23505' ? 'That defect key is already used in this project.' : error.message },
      { status: error.code === '23505' ? 409 : 400 }
    );
  }
  return NextResponse.json({ defect }, { status: 201 });
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\api\organizations\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import type { Database } from '@/types/supabase';

function createRequestSupabaseClient() {
  const cookieStore = cookies();

  return createServerClient<Database>(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );
}

export async function GET() {
  const supabase = createRequestSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (authError || !user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { data: memberships, error: membershipError } = await supabase
    .from('organization_members')
    .select('role, organization_id')
    .eq('user_id', user.id);

  if (membershipError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load organization memberships.' }, {
      status: 500,
      headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' },
    });
  }

  if (!memberships?.length) {
    return NextResponse.json({ organizations: [] }, { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
  }

  const organizationIds = ((memberships || []) as Array<{ organization_id: string; role?: string }>).map((membership) => membership.organization_id);
  const { data: organizations, error: organizationsError } = await supabase
    .from('organizations')
    .select('id, name, slug')
    .in('id', organizationIds)
    .order('name', { ascending: true });

  if (organizationsError || !organizations?.length) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load your organizations.' }, {
      status: 500,
      headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' },
    });
  }

  const organizationById = new Map(organizations.map((organization) => [organization.id, organization] as const));
  const result = memberships.map((membership) => ({
    role: membership.role,
    organization: organizationById.get(membership.organization_id) ?? null,
  }));

  return NextResponse.json({ organizations: result }, { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRequestSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (authError || !user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  if (!user.email) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'An email address is required to create an organization.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  let body: { name?: unknown; slug?: unknown };
  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Request body must be valid JSON.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const name = typeof body.name === 'string' ? body.name.trim() : '';
  const slug = typeof body.slug === 'string' ? body.slug.trim().toLowerCase() : '';

  if (!name || name.length > 255) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter an organization name up to 255 characters.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  if (!/^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$/.test(slug) || slug.length > 100) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Use a slug with lowercase letters, numbers, and single hyphens.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const serviceRoleKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY || process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  if (!supabaseUrl || !serviceRoleKey || /^(your|example|placeholder|replace|insert|put|change)[-_ ]/i.test(serviceRoleKey)) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Organization setup is unavailable: configure a real SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY on the server.' },
      { status: 503 }
    );
  }

  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey, {
    auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
  });

  const fullName = String(
    user.user_metadata?.full_name ||
    user.user_metadata?.name ||
    user.email?.split('@')[0] ||
    'QA User'
  ).trim();

  // Auth-created users may not have a matching profile row yet. The service role
  // can safely bootstrap that row and the first organization membership.
  const { error: profileError } = await admin.from('profiles').upsert(
    { id: user.id, email: user.email, full_name: fullName },
    { onConflict: 'id' }
  );

  if (profileError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: `Could not prepare your profile: ${profileError.message}` }, { status: 500 });
  }

  const { data: organization, error: organizationError } = await admin
    .from('organizations')
    .insert({ name, slug })
    .select()
    .single();

  if (organizationError || !organization) {
    const duplicate = organizationError?.code === '23505';
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: duplicate ? 'That organization slug is already in use.' : organizationError?.message || 'Could not create organization.' },
      { status: duplicate ? 409 : 400 }
    );
  }

  const { error: membershipError } = await admin.from('organization_members').insert({
    organization_id: organization.id,
    user_id: user.id,
    role: 'org_admin',
  });

  if (membershipError) {
    // Avoid leaving an organization with no administrator if membership setup fails.
    await admin.from('organizations').delete().eq('id', organization.id);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: `Could not add you as organization admin: ${membershipError.message}` }, { status: 500 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ organization }, { status: 201 });
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\api\organizations\[id]\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import type { Database } from '@/types/supabase';

export async function PATCH(request: NextRequest, { params }: { params: { id: string } }) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient<Database>(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { name } = await request.json();

  if (!name) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Name is required' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  // The hand-maintained Database type omits generated table metadata, so
  // Supabase infers this update as `never`; keep the cast scoped to this query.
  const organizationQuery = supabase.from('organizations') as any;
  const { data: org, error } = await organizationQuery
    .update({ name })
    .eq('id', params.id)
    .select()
    .single();

  if (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ organization: org });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest, { params }: { params: { id: string } }) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient<Database>(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const memberQuery = supabase.from('organization_members') as any;
  const { data: membership, error: membershipError } = await memberQuery
    .select('role')
    .eq('organization_id', params.id)
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (membershipError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not verify your organization access.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
  if (!membership) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Organization not found.' }, { status: 404 });
  }
  if (membership.role !== 'org_admin' && membership.role !== 'super_admin') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Only organization admins can add members.' }, { status: 403 });
  }

  let body: { email?: unknown; role?: unknown };
  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Request body must be valid JSON.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const email = typeof body.email === 'string' ? body.email.trim().toLowerCase() : '';
  const allowedRoles = ['qa_manager', 'qa_engineer', 'developer', 'viewer'] as const;
  const role = typeof body.role === 'string' && allowedRoles.includes(body.role as typeof allowedRoles[number])
    ? body.role as typeof allowedRoles[number]
    : 'qa_engineer';

  if (!email || email.length > 255 || !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Enter a valid email address for an existing account.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const serviceRoleKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY || process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  const serviceRoleKeyIsPlaceholder = !serviceRoleKey || /^(your|example|placeholder|replace|insert|put|change)[-_ ]/i.test(serviceRoleKey);
  if (!supabaseUrl || serviceRoleKeyIsPlaceholder) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: 'Set a real SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY (or legacy SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY) in .env.local, then restart the dev server to load organization members.' },
      { status: 503 }
    );
  }

  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey, {
    auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false },
  });
  const { data: profile, error: profileError } = await admin
    .from('profiles')
    .select('id')
    .ilike('email', email)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (profileError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not look up that account.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
  if (!profile) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'No registered account was found for that email. The person must register first.' }, { status: 404 });
  }

  const { error: addError } = await admin.from('organization_members').insert({
    organization_id: params.id,
    user_id: profile.id,
    role,
  });

  if (addError?.code === '23505') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'That user is already a member of this organization.' }, { status: 409 });
  }
  if (addError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: `Could not add the member: ${addError.message}` }, { status: 500 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ member: { email, role } }, { status: 201 });
}

export async function GET(_request: NextRequest, { params }: { params: { id: string } }) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient<Database>(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) { return cookieStore.get(name)?.value; },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) { cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options }); },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) { cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options }); },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const membershipQuery = supabase.from('organization_members') as any;
  const { data: membership, error: membershipError } = await membershipQuery
    .select('role')
    .eq('organization_id', params.id)
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (membershipError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not verify your organization access.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
  if (!membership) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Organization not found.' }, { status: 404 });
  }
  if (membership.role !== 'org_admin' && membership.role !== 'super_admin') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Only organization admins can view members.' }, { status: 403 });
  }

  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const serviceRoleKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY || process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  const serviceRoleKeyIsPlaceholder = !serviceRoleKey || /^(your|example|placeholder|replace|insert|put|change)[-_ ]/i.test(serviceRoleKey);
  if (!supabaseUrl || serviceRoleKeyIsPlaceholder) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Member management is unavailable: configure a real SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY on the server.' }, { status: 503 });
  }

  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey, {
    auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false, detectSessionInUrl: false },
  });
  const { data: rows, error: membersError } = await (admin.from('organization_members') as any)
    .select('user_id, role')
    .eq('organization_id', params.id);

  if (membersError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load organization members.' }, { status: 500 });
  }

  const memberships = (rows ?? []) as Array<{ user_id: string; role: string }>;
  if (memberships.length === 0) {
    return NextResponse.json({ members: [] }, { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
  }

  const { data: profiles, error: profilesError } = await (admin.from('profiles') as any)
    .select('id, email, full_name')
    .in('id', memberships.map((item) => item.user_id));

  if (profilesError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load member profiles.' }, { status: 500 });
  }

  const profileById = new Map<string, { id: string; email: string; full_name: string | null }>();
  for (const profile of (profiles ?? []) as Array<{ id: string; email: string; full_name: string | null }>) {
    profileById.set(profile.id, profile);
  }

  const members = memberships.map((item) => {
    const profile = profileById.get(item.user_id);
    return {
      userId: item.user_id,
      role: item.role,
      email: profile?.email ?? '',
      fullName: profile?.full_name || profile?.email || 'Unknown user',
      isCurrentUser: item.user_id === user.id,
    };
  });

  return NextResponse.json({ members }, { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
}

export async function DELETE(request: NextRequest, { params }: { params: { id: string } }) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient<Database>(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) { return cookieStore.get(name)?.value; },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) { cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options }); },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) { cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options }); },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const membershipQuery = supabase.from('organization_members') as any;
  const { data: membership, error: membershipError } = await membershipQuery
    .select('role')
    .eq('organization_id', params.id)
    .eq('user_id', user.id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (membershipError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not verify your organization access.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
  if (!membership) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Organization not found.' }, { status: 404 });
  }
  if (membership.role !== 'org_admin' && membership.role !== 'super_admin') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Only organization admins can deactivate members.' }, { status: 403 });
  }

  let body: { userId?: unknown };
  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Request body must be valid JSON.' }, { status: 400 });
  }
  const userId = typeof body.userId === 'string' ? body.userId : '';
  if (!userId) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'A member user ID is required.' }, { status: 400 });
  }
  if (userId === user.id) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'You cannot deactivate your own organization access.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL;
  const serviceRoleKey = process.env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY || process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY;
  const serviceRoleKeyIsPlaceholder = !serviceRoleKey || /^(your|example|placeholder|replace|insert|put|change)[-_ ]/i.test(serviceRoleKey);
  if (!supabaseUrl || serviceRoleKeyIsPlaceholder) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Member management is unavailable: configure a real SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY on the server.' }, { status: 503 });
  }

  const admin = createClient(supabaseUrl, serviceRoleKey, {
    auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false, detectSessionInUrl: false },
  });
  const adminMembershipQuery = admin.from('organization_members') as any;
  const { data: target, error: targetError } = await adminMembershipQuery
    .select('user_id, role')
    .eq('organization_id', params.id)
    .eq('user_id', userId)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (targetError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not look up that organization member.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
  if (!target) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'That user is not a member of this organization.' }, { status: 404 });
  }
  if (target.role === 'org_admin' || target.role === 'super_admin') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Organization administrators cannot be deactivated.' }, { status: 403 });
  }

  const { error: deactivateError } = await adminMembershipQuery
    .delete()
    .eq('organization_id', params.id)
    .eq('user_id', userId);
  if (deactivateError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not deactivate this organization member.' }, { status: 500 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ deactivated: true, userId });
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { Database } from '@/types/supabase';

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  // Get user's organizations
  const { data: memberships } = await supabase
    .from('organization_members')
    .select('organization_id')
    .eq('user_id', user.id);

  if (!memberships?.length) {
    return NextResponse.json({ projects: [] });
  }

  const orgIds = memberships.map(m => m.organization_id);

  // Fetch projects with RLS enforcement
  const { data: projects, error } = await supabase
    .from('projects')
    .select('*')
    .in('organization_id', orgIds)
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

  if (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ projects });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { key, name, description, organizationId } = await request.json();

  if (!key || !name || !organizationId) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Missing required fields' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const { data: project, error } = await supabase
  .from('projects')
  .insert([{
    key: key.toUpperCase(),
    name,
    description,
    organization_id: organizationId,
    status: 'active',
  }])
  .select()
  .single();

  if (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 400 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ project });
}

import { NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';

export async function GET(_request: Request, { params }: { params: { id: string } }) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  if (!/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(params.id)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Project not found.' }, { status: 404 });
  }

  const { data: project, error } = await supabase
    .from('projects')
    .select('*')
    .eq('id', params.id)
    .maybeSingle();

  if (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load this project.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
  if (!project) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Project not found.' }, { status: 404 });
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ project });
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\api\reports\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createRouteSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/route-client';

const reportTables = {
  projects: 'projects',
  requirements: 'requirements',
  testCases: 'test_cases',
  testPlans: 'test_plans',
  testRuns: 'test_runs',
  defects: 'defects',
} as const;

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRouteSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const kind = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get('type') as keyof typeof reportTables | null;
  const projectId = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get('projectId');
  if (!kind || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(reportTables, kind)) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Select a supported report type.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  let query: any;
  switch (kind) {
    case 'projects':
      query = supabase.from('projects').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
      break;
    case 'requirements':
      query = supabase.from('requirements').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
      break;
    case 'testCases':
      query = supabase.from('test_cases').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
      break;
    case 'testPlans':
      query = supabase.from('test_plans').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
      break;
    case 'testRuns':
      query = supabase.from('test_runs').select('*').order('started_at', { ascending: false });
      break;
    case 'defects':
      query = supabase.from('defects').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
      break;
  }

  if (projectId) {
    query = kind === 'projects' ? query.eq('id', projectId) : query.eq('project_id', projectId);
  }

  const { data, error } = await query;
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not generate this report.' }, { status: 500 });
  return NextResponse.json({ rows: data ?? [] });
}

import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { Database } from '@/types/supabase';

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const { searchParams } = new URL(request.url);
  const projectId = searchParams.get('projectId');

  let query = supabase.from('requirements').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });

  if (projectId) {
    query = query.eq('project_id', projectId);
  }

  const { data: requirements, error } = await query;

  if (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: error.message }, { status: 500 });
  }

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  let body: Record<string, unknown>;
  try { body = await request.json(); } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Request body must be valid JSON.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const projectId = typeof body.projectId === 'string' ? body.projectId : '';
  const reqKey = typeof body.reqKey === 'string' ? body.reqKey.trim().toUpperCase() : '';
  const title = typeof body.title === 'string' ? body.title.trim() : '';
  if (!projectId || !reqKey || !title) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Project, requirement key, and title are required.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const { data: project, error: projectError } = await supabase
    .from('projects').select('id, organization_id').eq('id', projectId).maybeSingle();
  if (projectError || !project) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Select a project you can access.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const validPriorities = ['low', 'medium', 'high', 'critical'];
  const validStatuses = ['draft', 'under_review', 'approved', 'implemented', 'verified', 'rejected'];
  const validTypes = ['functional', 'non_functional', 'business', 'performance', 'security'];
  const priority = typeof body.priority === 'string' && validPriorities.includes(body.priority) ? body.priority : 'medium';
  const status = typeof body.status === 'string' && validStatuses.includes(body.status) ? body.status : 'draft';
  const type = typeof body.type === 'string' && validTypes.includes(body.type) ? body.type : 'functional';

  const { data: requirement, error } = await supabase.from('requirements').insert([{
    project_id: project.id,
    organization_id: project.organization_id,
    req_key: reqKey,
    title,
    description: typeof body.description === 'string' ? body.description.trim() || null : null,
    priority,
    status,
    type,
    owner_id: user.id,
  }] as any).select().single();

  if (error) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error.code === '23505' ? 'That requirement key is already used in this project.' : error.message },
      { status: error.code === '23505' ? 409 : 400 }
    );
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ requirement }, { status: 201 });
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\api\test-cases\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createRouteSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/route-client';

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRouteSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const projectId = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get('projectId');
  let query = supabase.from('test_cases').select('*').order('created_at', { ascending: false });
  if (projectId) query = query.eq('project_id', projectId);

  const { data, error } = await query;
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load test cases.' }, { status: 500 });
  return NextResponse.json({ testCases: data ?? [] });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRouteSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  let body: Record<string, unknown>;
  try { body = await request.json(); } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Request body must be valid JSON.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const projectId = typeof body.projectId === 'string' ? body.projectId : '';
  const caseKey = typeof body.caseKey === 'string' ? body.caseKey.trim().toUpperCase() : '';
  const title = typeof body.title === 'string' ? body.title.trim() : '';
  const rawRequirementIds = Array.isArray(body.requirementIds) ? body.requirementIds : [];
  const requirementIds = [...new Set(rawRequirementIds.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string'))];
  if (!projectId || !caseKey || !title) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Project, test case key, and title are required.' }, { status: 400 });
  }
  if (rawRequirementIds.some((id) => typeof id !== 'string') || requirementIds.some((id) => !/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[1-8][0-9a-f]{3}-[89ab][0-9a-f]{3}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/i.test(id))) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'One or more linked requirements are invalid.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const { data: project, error: projectError } = await supabase
    .from('projects').select('id, organization_id').eq('id', projectId).maybeSingle();
  if (projectError || !project) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Select a project you can access.' }, { status: 400 });

  if (requirementIds.length) {
    const { data: requirements, error: requirementsError } = await supabase
      .from('requirements')
      .select('id')
      .eq('project_id', project.id)
      .in('id', requirementIds);
    if (requirementsError || requirements?.length !== requirementIds.length) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Choose requirements from the selected project.' }, { status: 400 });
    }
  }

  const { data: testCase, error } = await supabase.from('test_cases').insert({
    project_id: project.id,
    organization_id: project.organization_id,
    case_key: caseKey,
    title,
    description: typeof body.description === 'string' ? body.description.trim() || null : null,
    preconditions: typeof body.preconditions === 'string' ? body.preconditions.trim() || null : null,
    priority: typeof body.priority === 'string' ? body.priority : 'medium',
    test_type: typeof body.testType === 'string' ? body.testType : 'functional',
    category: typeof body.category === 'string' && body.category.trim() ? body.category.trim() : 'General',
    automation_status: typeof body.automationStatus === 'string' ? body.automationStatus : 'manual',
    author_id: user.id,
  }).select().single();

  if (error) {
    return NextResponse.json(
      { error: error.code === '23505' ? 'That test case key is already used in this project.' : error.message },
      { status: error.code === '23505' ? 409 : 400 }
    );
  }

  if (requirementIds.length) {
    const { error: linkError } = await supabase.from('test_case_requirements').insert(
      requirementIds.map((requirementId) => ({ test_case_id: testCase.id, requirement_id: requirementId }))
    );
    if (linkError) {
      await supabase.from('test_cases').delete().eq('id', testCase.id);
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not link the selected requirements to this test case.' }, { status: 400 });
    }
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ testCase }, { status: 201 });
}
