===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\api\test-runs\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createRouteSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/route-client';

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRouteSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  const projectId = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get('projectId');
  let query = supabase.from('test_runs').select('*').order('started_at', { ascending: false });
  if (projectId) query = query.eq('project_id', projectId);
  const { data, error } = await query;
  if (error) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load test runs.' }, { status: 500 });
  return NextResponse.json({ testRuns: data ?? [] });
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRouteSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });

  let body: Record<string, unknown>;
  try { body = await request.json(); } catch {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Request body must be valid JSON.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const projectId = typeof body.projectId === 'string' ? body.projectId : '';
  const name = typeof body.name === 'string' ? body.name.trim() : '';
  if (!projectId || !name) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Project and test run name are required.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const { data: project, error: projectError } = await supabase
    .from('projects').select('id, organization_id').eq('id', projectId).maybeSingle();
  if (projectError || !project) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Select a project you can access.' }, { status: 400 });

  let testPlanId: string | null = null;
  if (typeof body.testPlanId === 'string' && body.testPlanId) {
    const { data: plan } = await supabase.from('test_plans').select('id')
      .eq('id', body.testPlanId).eq('project_id', project.id).maybeSingle();
    if (!plan) return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Select a test plan from this project.' }, { status: 400 });
    testPlanId = plan.id;
  }

  const { data: run, error: runError } = await supabase.from('test_runs').insert({
    project_id: project.id,
    organization_id: project.organization_id,
    name,
    test_plan_id: testPlanId,
    assigned_to: user.id,
    status: 'in_progress',
  }).select().single();

  if (runError || !run) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: runError?.message || 'Could not create the test run.' }, { status: 400 });
  }

  const { data: cases, error: casesError } = await supabase.from('test_cases')
    .select('id').eq('project_id', project.id);
  if (casesError) {
    await supabase.from('test_runs').delete().eq('id', run.id);
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load test cases for this run.' }, { status: 500 });
  }

  if (cases?.length) {
    const { error: executionError } = await supabase.from('test_executions').insert(
      cases.map((testCase) => ({
        test_run_id: run.id,
        test_case_id: testCase.id,
        organization_id: project.organization_id,
        tester_id: user.id,
        status: 'not_run' as const,
      }))
    );
    if (executionError) {
      await supabase.from('test_runs').delete().eq('id', run.id);
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not initialize test executions for this run.' }, { status: 500 });
    }
  }

  return NextResponse.json({ testRun: run, testCaseCount: cases?.length ?? 0 }, { status: 201 });
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\api\traceability\route.ts =====
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';
import { createRouteSupabaseClient } from '@/lib/supabase/route-client';

const OPEN_DEFECT_STATUSES = new Set(['new', 'open', 'in_progress', 'ready_for_retest', 'reopened']);

export async function GET(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRouteSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (authError || !user) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 });
  }

  const projectId = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get('projectId');
  let requirementsQuery = supabase
    .from('requirements')
    .select('id, project_id, req_key, title, status')
    .order('created_at', { ascending: false });

  if (projectId) requirementsQuery = requirementsQuery.eq('project_id', projectId);

  const { data: requirements, error: requirementsError } = await requirementsQuery;
  if (requirementsError) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load requirements for the traceability matrix.' }, { status: 500 });
  }

  if (!requirements?.length) {
    return NextResponse.json({ requirements: [], summary: { totalRequirements: 0, coveredRequirements: 0, coveragePercentage: 0, openDefects: 0 } });
  }

  const requirementIds = requirements.map((requirement) => requirement.id);
  const projectIds = [...new Set(requirements.map((requirement) => requirement.project_id))];
  const [linksResult, projectsResult] = await Promise.all([
    supabase
      .from('test_case_requirements')
      .select('requirement_id, test_case_id')
      .in('requirement_id', requirementIds),
    supabase
      .from('projects')
      .select('id, name')
      .in('id', projectIds),
  ]);

  if (linksResult.error || projectsResult.error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load requirement links for the traceability matrix.' }, { status: 500 });
  }

  const links = linksResult.data ?? [];
  const linkedTestCaseIds = [...new Set(links.map((link) => link.test_case_id))];
  type TraceabilityTestCase = { id: string; project_id: string; case_key: string; title: string };
  type TraceabilityDefect = { id: string; test_case_id: string | null; status: string };
  let testCases: TraceabilityTestCase[] = [];
  let defects: TraceabilityDefect[] = [];

  if (linkedTestCaseIds.length) {
    const [testCasesResult, defectsResult] = await Promise.all([
      supabase
        .from('test_cases')
        .select('id, project_id, case_key, title')
        .in('id', linkedTestCaseIds),
      supabase
        .from('defects')
        .select('id, test_case_id, status')
        .in('test_case_id', linkedTestCaseIds),
    ]);

    if (testCasesResult.error || defectsResult.error) {
      return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not load linked test cases and defects.' }, { status: 500 });
    }

    testCases = testCasesResult.data ?? [];
    defects = defectsResult.data ?? [];
  }

  const projectsById = new Map((projectsResult.data ?? []).map((project) => [project.id, project.name] as const));
  const testCasesById = new Map(testCases.map((testCase) => [testCase.id, testCase] as const));
  const openDefectIdsByTestCaseId = new Map<string, Set<string>>();
  const allOpenDefectIds = new Set<string>();

  for (const defect of defects) {
    if (defect.test_case_id && OPEN_DEFECT_STATUSES.has(defect.status)) {
      const ids = openDefectIdsByTestCaseId.get(defect.test_case_id) ?? new Set<string>();
      ids.add(defect.id);
      openDefectIdsByTestCaseId.set(defect.test_case_id, ids);
    }
  }

  const traceabilityRows = requirements.map((requirement) => {
    const testCases = links
      .filter((link) => link.requirement_id === requirement.id)
      .flatMap((link) => {
        const testCase = testCasesById.get(link.test_case_id);
        // Ignore accidental cross-project links so the matrix never joins unrelated project data.
        if (!testCase || testCase.project_id !== requirement.project_id) return [];
        return [{ id: testCase.id, key: testCase.case_key, title: testCase.title }];
      });
    const testCaseIds = new Set(testCases.map((testCase) => testCase.id));
    const openDefectIds = new Set<string>();
    for (const testCaseId of testCaseIds) {
      for (const defectId of openDefectIdsByTestCaseId.get(testCaseId) ?? []) {
        openDefectIds.add(defectId);
        allOpenDefectIds.add(defectId);
      }
    }

    return {
      id: requirement.id,
      projectId: requirement.project_id,
      key: requirement.req_key,
      title: requirement.title,
      status: requirement.status,
      projectName: projectsById.get(requirement.project_id) ?? 'Project',
      testCases,
      openDefects: openDefectIds.size,
    };
  });

  const coveredRequirements = traceabilityRows.filter((row) => row.testCases.length > 0).length;
  const openDefects = allOpenDefectIds.size;

  return NextResponse.json({
    requirements: traceabilityRows,
    summary: {
      totalRequirements: traceabilityRows.length,
      coveredRequirements,
      coveragePercentage: Math.round((coveredRequirements / traceabilityRows.length) * 100),
      openDefects,
    },
  }, { headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } });
}

async function getLinkContext(request: NextRequest) {
  const supabase = createRouteSupabaseClient();
  const { data: { user }, error: authError } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  if (authError || !user) {
    return { response: NextResponse.json({ error: 'Unauthorized' }, { status: 401 }) };
  }

  let body: { requirementId?: unknown; testCaseId?: unknown };
  try {
    body = await request.json();
  } catch {
    return { response: NextResponse.json({ error: 'Request body must be valid JSON.' }, { status: 400 }) };
  }

  const requirementId = typeof body.requirementId === 'string' ? body.requirementId : '';
  const testCaseId = typeof body.testCaseId === 'string' ? body.testCaseId : '';
  if (!requirementId || !testCaseId) {
    return { response: NextResponse.json({ error: 'Requirement and test case are required.' }, { status: 400 }) };
  }

  const [requirementResult, testCaseResult] = await Promise.all([
    supabase.from('requirements').select('id, project_id').eq('id', requirementId).maybeSingle(),
    supabase.from('test_cases').select('id, project_id').eq('id', testCaseId).maybeSingle(),
  ]);

  if (requirementResult.error || testCaseResult.error || !requirementResult.data || !testCaseResult.data) {
    return { response: NextResponse.json({ error: 'Choose records you can access.' }, { status: 400 }) };
  }
  if (requirementResult.data.project_id !== testCaseResult.data.project_id) {
    return { response: NextResponse.json({ error: 'Requirements can only be linked to test cases in the same project.' }, { status: 400 }) };
  }

  return { supabase, requirementId, testCaseId };
}

export async function POST(request: NextRequest) {
  const context = await getLinkContext(request);
  if ('response' in context) return context.response;

  const { error } = await context.supabase.from('test_case_requirements').insert({
    requirement_id: context.requirementId,
    test_case_id: context.testCaseId,
  });

  if (error && error.code !== '23505') {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not link this test case to the requirement.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
  return NextResponse.json({ linked: true });
}

export async function DELETE(request: NextRequest) {
  const context = await getLinkContext(request);
  if ('response' in context) return context.response;

  const { error } = await context.supabase
    .from('test_case_requirements')
    .delete()
    .eq('requirement_id', context.requirementId)
    .eq('test_case_id', context.testCaseId);

  if (error) {
    return NextResponse.json({ error: 'Could not unlink this test case from the requirement.' }, { status: 500 });
  }
  return NextResponse.json({ linked: false });
}

'use client';

import { FormEvent, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';

export default function ForgotPasswordPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [sent, setSent] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const handleReset = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const redirectTo = `${window.location.origin}/auth/update-password`;
      const { error: resetError } = await supabase.auth.resetPasswordForEmail(email.trim(), {
        redirectTo,
      });

      if (resetError) {
        setError(resetError.message);
        return;
      }

      setSent(true);
    } catch {
      setError('Could not reach the authentication service. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-[#f0f2f5] px-4 py-12">
      {/* TestRail Brand Header */}
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded bg-[#48a43f] flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold text-lg shadow-sm">
          TR
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#1d2d3e] tracking-tight">QA Management Platform</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">Enterprise Test Management</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-lg shadow-sm p-8">
        <div className="mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e5e9ec]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[#1d2d3e]">Reset Password</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c] mt-0.5">
            {sent ? 'Check your email inbox' : 'Enter your email to receive a password reset link'}
          </p>
        </div>

        {sent ? (
          <div className="text-center py-4" role="status">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 bg-green-50 text-[#48a43f] border border-green-200 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3 text-xl font-bold">
              ?
            </div>
            <p className="text-sm text-[#1d2d3e] font-medium mb-1">Reset instructions sent</p>
            <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c] mb-6">
              If an account exists for <span className="font-semibold text-[#1d2d3e]">{email.trim()}</span>, you will receive an email shortly with instructions.
            </p>
            <Link
              href="/auth/login"
              className="inline-block px-4 py-2 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3d8c36] text-white font-semibold rounded text-sm shadow-sm transition"
            >
              Back to Sign In
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleReset} className="space-y-4">
            {error && (
              <div role="alert" className="p-3 rounded bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs font-medium">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <div>
              <label htmlFor="email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[#4f6174] uppercase tracking-wide mb-1.5">
                Email Address
              </label>
              <input
                id="email"
                type="email"
                value={email}
                onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-[#1d2d3e] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f] transition placeholder-[#95a5a6]"
                placeholder="you@company.com"
                autoComplete="email"
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="pt-2">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={loading}
                className="w-full py-2.5 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3d8c36] text-white font-semibold rounded text-sm shadow-sm transition disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {loading ? 'Sending link...' : 'Send Reset Link'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}

        <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-[#e5e9ec] text-center text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">
          Remember your password?{' '}
          <Link href="/auth/login" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline font-semibold">
            Return to log in
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-[#8c9ba5]">
        &copy; {new Date().getFullYear()} QA Management Platform. All rights reserved.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\auth\update-password\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { FormEvent, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { supabase } from '@/lib/supabase/client';

export default function UpdatePasswordPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [checkingSession, setCheckingSession] = useState(true);
  const [hasSession, setHasSession] = useState(false);
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    const checkSession = async () => {
      const { data, error: sessionError } = await supabase.auth.getSession();
      if (!active) return;

      setHasSession(Boolean(data.session) && !sessionError);
      setCheckingSession(false);
    };

    void checkSession();

    const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, session) => {
      if (!active) return;
      setHasSession(Boolean(session));
      setCheckingSession(false);
    });

    return () => {
      active = false;
      subscription.unsubscribe();
    };
  }, []);

  const handleSubmit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError('');

    if (password.length < 8) {
      setError('Your password must be at least 8 characters.');
      return;
    }

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError('The passwords do not match.');
      return;
    }

    setSaving(true);
    try {
      const { error: updateError } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({ password });
      if (updateError) {
        setError(updateError.message);
        return;
      }

      router.replace('/dashboard');
      router.refresh();
    } catch {
      setError('Could not update your password. Please request a new reset link and try again.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="min-h-screen flex flex-col items-center justify-center bg-[#f0f2f5] px-4 py-12">
      {/* TestRail Brand Header */}
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded bg-[#48a43f] flex items-center justify-center text-white font-bold text-lg shadow-sm">
          TR
        </div>
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#1d2d3e] tracking-tight">QA Management Platform</h1>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">Enterprise Test Management</p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="w-full max-w-md bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-lg shadow-sm p-8">
        <div className="mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e5e9ec]">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-[#1d2d3e]">Choose a New Password</h2>
          <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c] mt-0.5">Set a secure password for your account</p>
        </div>

        {checkingSession ? (
          <div className="py-6 text-center text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">Verifying your reset link...</div>
        ) : !hasSession ? (
          <div className="text-center py-4">
            <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c] mb-4">
              This reset link is invalid or has expired. Please request a new link to continue.
            </p>
            <Link
              href="/auth/forgot-password"
              className="inline-block px-4 py-2 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3d8c36] text-white font-semibold rounded text-sm shadow-sm transition"
            >
              Request Another Reset Link
            </Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-4">
            {error && (
              <div role="alert" className="p-3 rounded bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs font-medium">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <div>
              <label htmlFor="password" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[#4f6174] uppercase tracking-wide mb-1.5">
                New Password
              </label>
              <input
                id="password"
                type="password"
                value={password}
                onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-[#1d2d3e] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f] transition placeholder-[#95a5a6]"
                placeholder="••••••••"
                minLength={8}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label htmlFor="confirmPassword" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[#4f6174] uppercase tracking-wide mb-1.5">
                Confirm Password
              </label>
              <input
                id="confirmPassword"
                type="password"
                value={confirmPassword}
                onChange={(event) => setConfirmPassword(event.target.value)}
                className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-[#1d2d3e] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f] transition placeholder-[#95a5a6]"
                placeholder="••••••••"
                minLength={8}
                required
              />
            </div>

            <div className="pt-2">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={saving}
                className="w-full py-2.5 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3d8c36] text-white font-semibold rounded text-sm shadow-sm transition disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {saving ? 'Updating password...' : 'Update Password'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}

        <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-[#e5e9ec] text-center text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">
          <Link href="/auth/login" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline font-semibold">
            Back to Sign In
          </Link>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mt-8 text-center text-xs text-[#8c9ba5]">
        &copy; {new Date().getFullYear()} QA Management Platform. All rights reserved.
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { useAuth } from '@/components/auth/AuthProvider';
import { FolderKanban, FileText, CheckSquare, TrendingUp, AlertTriangle, AlertOctagon } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';

type DashboardData = {
  stats: {
    projectCount: number;
    requirementCount: number;
    testCaseCount: number;
    passRate: number | null;
    openDefectCount: number;
    criticalDefectCount: number;
  };
  executionProgress: { id: string; name: string; progress: number | null; total: number }[];
  recentDefects: { id: string; defect_key: string; summary: string; severity: string; status: string }[];
};

export default function DashboardPage() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const [data, setData] = useState<DashboardData | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const loadDashboard = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/dashboard', { cache: 'no-store' });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'Could not load dashboard data.');
      setData(result as DashboardData);
      setError('');
    } catch (loadError: unknown) {
      setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load dashboard data.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    void loadDashboard();
    const refresh = () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') void loadDashboard(); };
    window.addEventListener('focus', refresh);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', refresh);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('focus', refresh);
      document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', refresh);
    };
  }, [loadDashboard]);

  const displayName = String(
    user?.user_metadata?.full_name || user?.user_metadata?.name || user?.email?.split('@')[0] || 'there'
  ).trim().split(/\s+/)[0];

  const stats = [
    {
      label: 'Total Projects',
      value: data?.stats.projectCount.toString() ?? (loading ? '…' : '—'),
      icon: FolderKanban,
      accent: 'border-t-4 border-[#276fbf]',
      iconColor: 'text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff]',
      href: '/projects',
    },
    {
      label: 'Total Requirements',
      value: data?.stats.requirementCount.toString() ?? (loading ? '…' : '—'),
      icon: FileText,
      accent: 'border-t-4 border-indigo-500',
      iconColor: 'text-indigo-600 dark:text-indigo-400',
      href: '/requirements',
    },
    {
      label: 'Test Cases',
      value: data?.stats.testCaseCount.toString() ?? (loading ? '…' : '—'),
      icon: CheckSquare,
      accent: 'border-t-4 border-[#48a43f]',
      iconColor: 'text-[#48a43f]',
      href: '/test-cases',
    },
    {
      label: 'Pass Rate',
      value: data?.stats.passRate == null ? '—' : `${data.stats.passRate}%`,
      icon: TrendingUp,
      accent: 'border-t-4 border-emerald-500',
      iconColor: 'text-emerald-600 dark:text-emerald-400',
      href: '/test-runs',
    },
    {
      label: 'Open Defects',
      value: data?.stats.openDefectCount.toString() ?? (loading ? '…' : '—'),
      icon: AlertTriangle,
      accent: 'border-t-4 border-amber-500',
      iconColor: 'text-amber-600 dark:text-amber-400',
      href: '/defects',
    },
    {
      label: 'Critical Defects',
      value: data?.stats.criticalDefectCount.toString() ?? (loading ? '…' : '—'),
      icon: AlertOctagon,
      accent: 'border-t-4 border-red-500',
      iconColor: 'text-red-600 dark:text-red-400',
      href: '/defects',
    },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9] dark:bg-[#101622] text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 transition-colors">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {/* Welcome Sub-header */}
          <div className="mb-6 flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-2 pb-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#222e3e]">
            <div>
              <h2 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">Welcome back, {displayName}</h2>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Here&apos;s what&apos;s happening across your testing suites and test runs today.</p>
            </div>
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <Link
                href="/test-runs/new"
                className="bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-1.5 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors"
              >
                + Start Test Run
              </Link>
            </div>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div role="alert" className="mb-5 p-3 text-xs bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900 rounded-md font-medium">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {/* Metrics Cards */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 xl:grid-cols-6 gap-4 mb-6">
            {stats.map((stat) => {
              const Icon = stat.icon;
              return (
                <Link
                  key={stat.label}
                  href={stat.href}
                  className={`bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] rounded-md border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] p-4 shadow-sm hover:shadow transition-all ${stat.accent} flex flex-col justify-between`}
                >
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">{stat.label}</p>
                    <Icon className={`w-4 h-4 ${stat.iconColor}`} />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white mt-2 tracking-tight">{stat.value}</p>
                </Link>
              );
            })}
          </div>

          {/* Two Column Section */}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-6">
            {/* Test Execution Progress */}
            <section className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
              <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] dark:bg-[#141d28] px-4 py-3 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d] flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">Test Execution Progress</h3>
                <Link href="/test-runs" className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] hover:underline">
                  View all runs ?
                </Link>
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 flex-1">
                {!data?.executionProgress.length ? (
                  <div className="text-center py-8">
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">No active test runs recorded.</p>
                    <Link
                      href="/test-runs/new"
                      className="mt-3 inline-block text-xs font-semibold text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] hover:underline"
                    >
                      + Create a test run
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-4">
                    {data.executionProgress.map((run) => (
                      <div key={run.id} className="p-2.5 rounded hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-[#1f2c3f] transition-colors">
                        <div className="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1.5 gap-4">
                          <span className="font-medium text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200 truncate">{run.name}</span>
                          <span className="font-semibold text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 shrink-0">
                            {run.progress == null ? '0%' : `${run.progress}%`}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                        <div className="h-2 bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700">
                          <div
                            className="h-full bg-[#48a43f] rounded-full transition-all"
                            style={{ width: `${run.progress ?? 0}%` }}
                          />
                        </div>
                      </div>
                    ))}
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </section>

            {/* Recent Defects */}
            <section className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden flex flex-col">
              <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] dark:bg-[#141d28] px-4 py-3 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d] flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-200">Recent Defects</h3>
                <Link href="/defects" className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] hover:underline">
                  View defect tracker ?
                </Link>
              </div>

              <div className="p-4 flex-1">
                {!data?.recentDefects.length ? (
                  <div className="text-center py-8">
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">No defects reported.</p>
                    <Link
                      href="/defects/new"
                      className="mt-3 inline-block text-xs font-semibold text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] hover:underline"
                    >
                      + Log a new defect
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <div className="space-y-2.5">
                    {data.recentDefects.map((defect) => {
                      const severity = defect.severity.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + defect.severity.slice(1);
                      const severityClass = ['critical', 'blocker'].includes(defect.severity)
                        ? 'bg-[#f2dede] dark:bg-[#34161a] text-[#a94442] dark:text-[#ff7875] border border-[#ebccd1] dark:border-[#521e24]'
                        : defect.severity === 'major'
                        ? 'bg-[#fcf8e3] dark:bg-[#332711] text-[#8a6d3b] dark:text-[#faad14] border border-[#faebcc] dark:border-[#534019]'

                      return (

                            {severity}
                          </span>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                )}

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\defects\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { Plus, Bug } from 'lucide-react';

type Defect = { id: string; defect_key: string; summary: string; project_id: string; severity: string; priority: string; status: string };
type Project = { id: string; name: string };

export default function DefectsPage() {
  const [defects, setDefects] = useState<Defect[]>([]);
  const [projectNames, setProjectNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([fetch('/api/defects', { cache: 'no-store' }), fetch('/api/projects', { cache: 'no-store' })])
      .then(async ([defectsResponse, projectsResponse]) => {
        const [defectsData, projectsData] = await Promise.all([defectsResponse.json(), projectsResponse.json()]);
        if (!defectsResponse.ok) throw new Error(defectsData.error || 'Could not load defects.');
        if (!projectsResponse.ok) throw new Error(projectsData.error || 'Could not load projects.');
        setDefects(defectsData.defects ?? []);
        setProjectNames(Object.fromEntries((projectsData.projects as Project[]).map((project) => [project.id, project.name])));
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load defects.'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const getSeverityBadge = (severity: string) => {
    switch (severity?.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'blocker':
      case 'critical':
        return 'bg-[#f2dede] dark:bg-[#34161a] text-[#a94442] dark:text-[#ff7875] border border-[#ebccd1] dark:border-[#521e24]';
      case 'major':
        return 'bg-[#fcf8e3] dark:bg-[#332711] text-[#8a6d3b] dark:text-[#faad14] border border-[#faebcc] dark:border-[#534019]';
      case 'minor':
        return 'bg-[#e0f2fe] dark:bg-[#112d44] text-[#0369a1] dark:text-[#38bdf8] border border-[#bae6fd] dark:border-[#1e4d6d]';
      case 'trivial':
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700';
    }
  };

  const getStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status?.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'fixed':
      case 'closed':
        return 'bg-[#dff0d8] dark:bg-[#132c1c] text-[#3c763d] dark:text-[#52c41a] border border-[#d6e9c6] dark:border-[#1d4c2b]';
      case 'in_progress':
        return 'bg-[#e0f2fe] dark:bg-[#112d44] text-[#0369a1] dark:text-[#38bdf8] border border-[#bae6fd] dark:border-[#1e4d6d]';
      case 'open':
      case 'new':
        return 'bg-[#fcf8e3] dark:bg-[#332711] text-[#8a6d3b] dark:text-[#faad14] border border-[#faebcc] dark:border-[#534019]';
      case 'rejected':
        return 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700 line-through';
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9] dark:bg-[#101622] text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 transition-colors">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {/* Header row */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">Defects</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                Log, prioritize, and monitor defects discovered during test execution cycles
              </p>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/defects/new"
              className="bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 focus:outline-none"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.5]" />
              <span>Add Defect</span>
            </Link>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900 rounded text-xs font-medium">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {loading ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 shadow-sm">
              Loading defects...
            </div>
          ) : defects.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-10 text-center shadow-sm">
              <Bug className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">No defects reported</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                No active bugs or issues found. All test runs are currently executing smoothly!
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/defects/new"
                className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />
                Log a new defect
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full min-w-[780px] text-left">
                  <thead className="bg-[#f8fafc] dark:bg-[#141d28] border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Key</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Summary</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Project</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Severity</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Priority</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Status</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#edf2f7] dark:divide-[#222e3e]">
                    {defects.map((defect) => (
                      <tr key={defect.id} className="hover:bg-[#f8fafc] dark:hover:bg-[#1f2c3f] transition-colors">
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className="font-mono font-bold text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] bg-[#eef5fc] dark:bg-[#172739] border border-[#d2e3f5] dark:border-[#233f5d] px-2 py-0.5 rounded cursor-pointer hover:underline">
                            {defect.defect_key}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                          {defect.summary}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                          {projectNames[defect.project_id] || 'General'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className={`inline-block text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize ${getSeverityBadge(defect.severity)}`}>
                            {defect.severity}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 capitalize">
                          {defect.priority}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className={`inline-block text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize ${getStatusBadge(defect.status)}`}>
                            {defect.status.replace('_', ' ')}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\defects\new\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { ProjectSelector, type ProjectOption } from '@/components/forms/project-selector';
import { ArrowLeft, Bug } from 'lucide-react';

export default function NewDefectPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [project, setProject] = useState<ProjectOption | null>(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ defectKey: '', summary: '', description: '', severity: 'major', priority: 'medium', environment: '', stepsToReproduce: '', expectedResult: '', actualResult: '' });
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const submit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!project) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/defects', {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...form, projectId: project.id }),
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'Could not report the defect.');
      router.push('/defects');
    } catch (submitError: unknown) {
      setError(submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : 'Could not report the defect.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto">
            {/* Breadcrumb row */}

                        placeholder="e.g. Session expires immediately when switching tabs"
                        required
                        maxLength={255}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"

                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\organizations\new\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { Building2, Plus, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';
import Link from 'next/link';

export default function NewOrganizationPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [slug, setSlug] = useState('');
  const [slugEdited, setSlugEdited] = useState(false);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const handleNameChange = (value: string) => {
    setName(value);
    if (!slugEdited) {
      setSlug(value.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, ''));
    }
  };

  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/organizations', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name, slug }),
      });
      const result = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        setError(result.error || 'Could not create the organization.');
        return;
      }

      router.push('/projects/new');
      router.refresh();
    } catch {
      setError('Could not reach the server. Check your connection and try again.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            {/* Breadcrumb */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-[#6e7d8c] mb-1">
              <Link href="/settings" className="hover:underline">Administration</Link>
              <span>/</span>
              <span className="text-[#1d2d3e] font-medium">New Organization</span>
            </div>

            <div className="mb-5 flex items-center justify-between">
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#1d2d3e] flex items-center gap-2">
                  <Building2 className="w-5 h-5 text-[#48a43f]" />
                  Create Organization
                </h1>
                <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c] mt-0.5">
                  Organizations isolate your projects, repositories, and test runs
                </p>
              </div>
              <Link
                href="/settings"
                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] hover:bg-[#f5f7f9] text-[#4f6174] text-xs font-semibold rounded shadow-sm transition"
              >
                <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                Cancel
              </Link>
            </div>

            {error && (
              <div className="mb-5 p-3 rounded bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs font-medium">
                {error}
              </div>
            )}

            <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 space-y-5">
              <div>
                <label htmlFor="organization-name" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[#4f6174] uppercase tracking-wide mb-1.5">
                  Organization Name
                </label>
                <input
                  id="organization-name"
                  type="text"
                  value={name}
                  onChange={(event) => handleNameChange(event.target.value)}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-[#1d2d3e] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f] transition placeholder-[#95a5a6]"
                  placeholder="e.g. Acme QA Labs"
                  maxLength={255}
                  required
                />
              </div>

              <div>
                <label htmlFor="organization-slug" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[#4f6174] uppercase tracking-wide mb-1.5">
                  Organization Slug (Identifier)
                </label>
                <input
                  id="organization-slug"
                  type="text"
                  value={slug}
                  onChange={(event) => {
                    setSlugEdited(true);
                    setSlug(event.target.value.toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9-]/g, '').replace(/-+/g, '-').replace(/^-|-$/g, ''));
                  }}
                  className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm font-mono text-[#1d2d3e] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f] transition placeholder-[#95a5a6]"
                  placeholder="acme-qa-labs"
                  maxLength={100}
                  pattern="[a-z0-9]+(-[a-z0-9]+)*"
                  required
                />
                <p className="text-[11px] text-[#6e7d8c] mt-1">
                  Used in routing and unique identifiers. Lowercase letters, numbers, and hyphens only.
                </p>
              </div>

              <div className="pt-3 border-t border-[#e5e9ec] flex items-center justify-end gap-3">
                <Link
                  href="/settings"
                  className="px-4 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] hover:bg-[#f5f7f9] text-[#4f6174] text-xs font-semibold rounded shadow-sm transition"
                >
                  Cancel
                </Link>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={loading}
                  className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 px-4 py-2 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3d8c36] text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded shadow-sm transition disabled:opacity-50"
                >
                  <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  {loading ? 'Creating...' : 'Create Organization'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\profile\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { FormEvent, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { useAuth } from '@/components/auth/AuthProvider';
import { User, Mail, Shield, Check, AlertCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ProfilePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const { user, loading: authLoading } = useAuth();
  const [fullName, setFullName] = useState('');
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    setFullName(String(user?.user_metadata?.full_name || user?.user_metadata?.name || ''));
    setEmail(user?.email || '');
  }, [user?.id, user?.email, user?.user_metadata]);

  const handleSubmit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!user) return;

    setSaving(true);
    setError('');
    setMessage('');

    try {
      const { supabase } = await import('@/lib/supabase/client');
      const nameChanged = fullName.trim() !== String(user.user_metadata?.full_name || user.user_metadata?.name || '');
      const emailChanged = email.trim().toLowerCase() !== (user.email || '').toLowerCase();

      if (!nameChanged && !emailChanged) {
        setMessage('Your profile is already up to date.');
        return;
      }

      const { error: updateError } = await supabase.auth.updateUser({
        ...(emailChanged ? { email: email.trim() } : {}),
        ...(nameChanged ? { data: { ...user.user_metadata, full_name: fullName.trim() } } : {}),
      });

      if (updateError) {
        setError(updateError.message);
        return;
      }

      setMessage(emailChanged
        ? 'Profile saved. Check your inbox to confirm the new email address.'
        : 'Profile saved successfully.');
    } catch {
      setError('Could not reach the authentication service. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-2xl">
            {/* Breadcrumb */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-[#6e7d8c] mb-1">
              <span>Administration</span>
              <span>/</span>
              <span className="text-[#1d2d3e] font-medium">My Profile</span>
            </div>

            <div className="mb-5">
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-[#1d2d3e] flex items-center gap-2">
                <User className="w-5 h-5 text-[#276fbf]" />
                User Profile
              </h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c] mt-0.5">
                Manage your personal information, credentials, and notification email
              </p>
            </div>

            {authLoading ? (
              <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-lg p-6 text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">
                Loading profile information...
              </div>
            ) : !user ? (
              <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-lg p-6 text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">
                Please sign in to manage your profile.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-lg shadow-sm p-6 space-y-5">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-4 pb-4 border-b border-[#e5e9ec]">
                  <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-full bg-[#1d2d3e] text-white flex items-center justify-center font-bold text-lg shadow-sm">
                    {fullName ? fullName.split(' ').map(n => n[0]).join('').slice(0, 2).toUpperCase() : 'JD'}
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-[#1d2d3e]">{fullName || 'User'}</h3>
                    <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">{email}</p>
                    <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 mt-1 px-2 py-0.5 rounded text-[10px] font-semibold bg-blue-50 text-[#276fbf] border border-blue-200">
                      <Shield className="w-3 h-3" />
                      Active Member
                    </span>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {message && (
                  <div className="p-3 rounded bg-green-50 border border-green-200 text-[#3d8c36] text-xs font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Check className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                    <span>{message}</span>
                  </div>
                )}

                {error && (
                  <div className="p-3 rounded bg-red-50 border border-red-200 text-red-700 text-xs font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
                    <AlertCircle className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                    <span>{error}</span>
                  </div>
                )}

                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="profile-name" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[#4f6174] uppercase tracking-wide mb-1.5">
                    Full Name
                  </label>
                  <input
                    id="profile-name"
                    value={fullName}
                    onChange={(event) => setFullName(event.target.value)}
                    className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-[#1d2d3e] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f] transition"
                    autoComplete="name"
                    maxLength={255}
                    required
                  />
                </div>

                <div>
                  <label htmlFor="profile-email" className="block text-xs font-semibold text-[#4f6174] uppercase tracking-wide mb-1.5">
                    Email Address
                  </label>
                  <div className="relative">
                    <input
                      id="profile-email"
                      type="email"
                      value={email}
                      onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 pl-9 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-[#1d2d3e] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f] transition"
                      autoComplete="email"
                      required
                    />
                    <Mail className="w-4 h-4 text-[#95a5a6] absolute left-3 top-2.5" />
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-[#6e7d8c] mt-1">
                    Changing your email address requires confirmation via a verification link sent to the new address.
                  </p>
                </div>

                <div className="pt-2 border-t border-[#e5e9ec] flex justify-end">
                  <button
                    type="submit"
                    disabled={saving}
                    className="px-5 py-2 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3d8c36] text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded shadow-sm transition disabled:opacity-50"
                  >
                    {saving ? 'Saving changes...' : 'Save Profile Changes'}
                  </button>
                </div>
              </form>
            )}
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState, useEffect } from 'react';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { FolderKanban, Plus } from 'lucide-react';

export default function ProjectsPage() {
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    fetch('/api/projects')
      .then((res) => res.json())
      .then((data) => {
        setProjects(data.projects || []);
        setLoading(false);
      });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9] dark:bg-[#101622] text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 transition-colors">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {/* Header row */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">

              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">Projects</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                Manage all your testing projects, milestone suites, and repositories

            <Link
              href="/projects/new"

            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.5]" />
              <span>Add Project</span>
            </Link>
          </div>

          {loading ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
              Loading projects...
            </div>
          ) : projects.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-10 text-center shadow-sm">
              <FolderKanban className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">No projects found</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                Get started by creating your first testing project to store requirements and test cases.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/projects/new"
                className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />
                Create your first project
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-5">
              {projects.map((project: any) => (
                <div
                  key={project.id}
                  className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-5 shadow-sm hover:shadow hover:border-[#b0c4de] dark:hover:border-[#3a4e6b] transition-all flex flex-col justify-between"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] bg-[#eef5fc] dark:bg-[#172739] border border-[#d2e3f5] dark:border-[#233f5d] px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                          {project.key}
                        </span>
                        <span
                          className={`text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize ${
                            project.status === 'active'
                              ? 'bg-[#dff0d8] dark:bg-[#132c1c] text-[#3c763d] dark:text-[#52c41a] border border-[#d6e9c6] dark:border-[#1d4c2b]'
                              : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700'
                          }`}
                        >
                          {project.status}
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">
                      {project.name}
                    </h3>

                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 mt-2 line-clamp-2 leading-relaxed">
                      {project.description || 'No description provided.'}
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-5 pt-3 border-t border-[#edf2f7] dark:border-[#222e3e] flex items-center justify-between">
                    <Link
                      href={`/projects/${project.id}`}
                      className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] hover:text-[#1d5ea8] dark:hover:text-[#7bb7ff] hover:underline inline-flex items-center gap-1"
                    >
                      View Project Details ?
                    </Link>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\projects\new\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { FolderPlus, ArrowLeft } from 'lucide-react';

type OrganizationMembership = {
  organization: { id: string; name: string } | null;
};

export default function NewProjectPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({ key: '', name: '', description: '', organizationId: '' });
  const [orgs, setOrgs] = useState<OrganizationMembership[]>([]);
  const [loadingOrganizations, setLoadingOrganizations] = useState(true);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [organizationLoadError, setOrganizationLoadError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetch('/api/organizations', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not load organizations.');
        return data;
      })
      .then((data) => {
        if (!active) return;
        if (!Array.isArray(data.organizations)) throw new Error('Organization response was invalid.');
        const memberships = (data.organizations ?? []).filter(
          (item: OrganizationMembership) => item.organization
        ) as OrganizationMembership[];
        setOrgs(memberships);
        if (memberships.length > 0) {
          setFormData((current) => ({ ...current, organizationId: memberships[0].organization!.id }));
        }
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => {
        if (active) setOrganizationLoadError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load organizations.');
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoadingOrganizations(false);
      });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, []);

  const handleSubmit = async (event: React.FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/projects', {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify(formData),
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
      const result = await response.json();

      if (!response.ok) {
        setError(result.error || 'Failed to create the project.');
        return;
      }

      router.push('/projects');
    } catch {
      setError('Could not reach the server. Check your connection and try again.');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            {/* Breadcrumb Navigation */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">
              <Link href="/projects" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
                <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Projects
              </Link>
              <span>/</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 font-medium">Add Project</span>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden mb-6">
              <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-[#276fbf]/10 text-[#276fbf] flex items-center justify-center">
                  <FolderPlus className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Add New Project</h1>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Create a repository for your test suites, runs, and cases</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="p-6">
                {error && (
                  <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs font-medium">
                    {error}
                  </div>
                )}
                {organizationLoadError && (
                  <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs font-medium">
                    {organizationLoadError}
                  </div>
                )}

                {loadingOrganizations ? (
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 py-4 text-center">Loading organizations…</p>
                ) : organizationLoadError ? (
                  <div className="text-center py-4">
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-600">We could not confirm your organization access, so project creation is paused.</p>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
                      className="mt-3 btn-testrail-secondary"
                    >
                      Try again
                    </button>
                  </div>
                ) : orgs.length === 0 ? (
                  <div className="text-center py-4">
                    <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Create an organization first</h2>
                    <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                      Projects belong to an organization. Create one to get started; you will be its admin.
                    </p>
                    <div className="mt-4 flex justify-center gap-3">
                      <Link
                        href="/organizations/new"
                        className="btn-testrail-primary"
                      >
                        Create Organization
                      </Link>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => router.push('/projects')}
                        className="btn-testrail-secondary"
                      >
                        Cancel
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <form onSubmit={handleSubmit} className="space-y-5">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="project-organization" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Organization <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                      </label>
                      <select
                        id="project-organization"
                        value={formData.organizationId}
                        onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, organizationId: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                        required
                        disabled={loadingOrganizations || orgs.length === 0}
                      >
                        {loadingOrganizations ? <option value="">Loading organizations...</option> : null}
                        {orgs.map((membership) => membership.organization && (
                          <option key={membership.organization.id} value={membership.organization.id}>
                            {membership.organization.name}
                          </option>
                        ))}
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="project-key" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Project Identifier Key <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="project-key"
                        type="text"
                        value={formData.key}
                        onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, key: event.target.value.toUpperCase() })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm font-mono text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                        placeholder="e.g. MBA, CRM, WEB"
                        maxLength={10}
                        required
                      />
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1">Short uppercase prefix for test case numbers (e.g. MBA-C1, MBA-C2)</p>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="project-name" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Project Name <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="project-name"
                        type="text"
                        value={formData.name}
                        onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, name: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                        placeholder="e.g. Mobile Banking App v2"
                        required
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="project-description" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Announcement / Description
                      </label>
                      <textarea
                        id="project-description"
                        value={formData.description}
                        onChange={(event) => setFormData({ ...formData, description: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                        placeholder="Brief overview of project scope, test environment, and goals"
                        rows={3}
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div className="pt-4 border-t border-[#edf2f7] flex items-center gap-3">
                      <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={loading || loadingOrganizations || orgs.length === 0}
                        className="btn-testrail-primary"
                      >
                        {loading ? 'Creating...' : 'Add Project'}
                      </button>
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => router.push('/projects')}
                        disabled={loading}
                        className="btn-testrail-secondary"
                      >
                        Cancel
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </form>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
