'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useParams } from 'next/navigation';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { FolderKanban, ArrowLeft, CheckSquare, PlayCircle, Plus } from 'lucide-react';

type Project = {
  id: string;
  key: string;
  name: string;
  description: string | null;
  status: string;
  created_at: string;
};

export default function ProjectDetailsPage() {
  const params = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const projectId = params.id;
  const [project, setProject] = useState<Project | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    fetch(`/api/projects/${encodeURIComponent(projectId)}`, { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const result = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'Could not load this project.');
        return result.project as Project;
      })
      .then((result) => { if (active) setProject(result); })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => {
        if (active) setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load this project.');
      })
      .finally(() => { if (active) setLoading(false); });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [projectId]);

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-4xl mx-auto">
            {/* Breadcrumb row */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-5">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500">
                <Link href="/projects" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
                  <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Projects
                </Link>
                <span>/</span>
                <span className="text-slate-800 font-semibold">{project?.name || 'Project Details'}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Link href="/projects" className="btn-testrail-secondary text-xs">
                  Back to Projects
                </Link>
                <Link href="/projects/new" className="btn-testrail-primary text-xs">
                  <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> New Project
                </Link>
              </div>
            </div>

            {error ? (
              <section role="alert" className="bg-white border border-red-200 rounded-md p-6 shadow-sm">
                <p className="text-sm font-semibold text-red-600">{error}</p>
                <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-slate-500">The project may have been removed or may belong to an organization you cannot access.</p>
              </section>
            ) : project ? (
              <div className="space-y-6">
                {/* Main Project Overview Card */}
                <section className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                      <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-[#276fbf] bg-[#eef5fc] border border-[#d2e3f5] px-2.5 py-1 rounded">
                        {project.key}
                      </span>
                      <h1 className="text-lg font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">{project.name}</h1>
                    </div>
                    <span
                      className={`text-xs px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full font-semibold capitalize ${
                        project.status === 'active'
                          ? 'bg-[#dff0d8] text-[#3c763d] border border-[#d6e9c6]'
                          : 'bg-slate-100 text-slate-600 border border-slate-200'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {project.status}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-6">
                    <h4 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 mb-2">Description / Scope</h4>
                    <p className="text-xs text-slate-700 whitespace-pre-wrap leading-relaxed">
                      {project.description || 'No project description provided.'}
                    </p>

                    {/* TestRail Quick Actions Grid */}
                    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-2 gap-4 mt-6 pt-6 border-t border-[#edf2f7]">

                        </div>
                      </Link>

                      <Link
                        href="/test-runs"
                        className="p-4 rounded border border-[#d8dee4] hover:border-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#f3f9f2]/40 transition-all flex items-start gap-3"
                      >
                        <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded bg-[#e8f5e9] text-[#48a43f] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                          <PlayCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </div>

                          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Execute suites and record test results</p>

                    </div>

                    <div className="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-[#edf2f7] flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                      <span>Created on {new Date(project.created_at).toLocaleDateString()}</span>
                      <span>ID: {project.id}</span>
                    </div>

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\reports\generate\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { ArrowLeft, FileSpreadsheet, Download } from 'lucide-react';

const reportTypes = [
  { value: 'projects', label: 'Projects' },
  { value: 'requirements', label: 'Requirements' },
  { value: 'testCases', label: 'Test Cases' },
  { value: 'testPlans', label: 'Test Plans' },
  { value: 'testRuns', label: 'Test Runs' },
  { value: 'defects', label: 'Defects' },
];

type Project = { id: string; key: string; name: string };
type ReportRow = Record<string, unknown>;

function csvValue(value: unknown) {
  const raw = value == null ? '' : typeof value === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(value) : String(value);
  const safe = /^[=+\-@\t\r]/.test(raw) ? `'${raw}` : raw;
  return `"${safe.replace(/"/g, '""')}"`;
}

export default function GenerateReportPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [type, setType] = useState('projects');
  const [projectId, setProjectId] = useState('');
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<Project[]>([]);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<ReportRow[]>([]);
  const [loadingProjects, setLoadingProjects] = useState(true);
  const [generating, setGenerating] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    const requestedType = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search).get('type');
    if (requestedType && reportTypes.some((item) => item.value === requestedType)) setType(requestedType);

    fetch('/api/projects', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not load projects.');
        setProjects(data.projects ?? []);
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load projects.'))
      .finally(() => setLoadingProjects(false));
  }, []);

  const generate = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    setGenerating(true);
    setError('');
    setMessage('');
    setRows([]);

    try {
      const search = new URLSearchParams({ type });
      if (projectId) search.set('projectId', projectId);
      const response = await fetch(`/api/reports?${search.toString()}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not generate this report.');

      const reportRows = (data.rows ?? []) as ReportRow[];
      setRows(reportRows.slice(0, 10));
      if (reportRows.length === 0) {
        setMessage('No records matched those filters.');
        return;
      }

      const columns = Object.keys(reportRows[0]);
      const csv = [columns.map(csvValue).join(','), ...reportRows.map((row) => columns.map((column) => csvValue(row[column])).join(','))].join('\r\n');
      const blob = new Blob([csv], { type: 'text/csv;charset=utf-8' });
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
      const anchor = document.createElement('a');
      const label = reportTypes.find((item) => item.value === type)?.label.toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, '-') || 'report';
      anchor.href = url;
      anchor.download = `qa-${label}-${new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)}.csv`;
      anchor.click();
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
      setMessage(`Generated ${reportRows.length} rows and downloaded the CSV report.`);
    } catch (generateError: unknown) {
      setError(generateError instanceof Error ? generateError.message : 'Could not generate this report.');
    } finally {
      setGenerating(false);
    }
  };

  const columns = rows[0] ? Object.keys(rows[0]).slice(0, 5) : [];

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto">
            {/* Breadcrumb row */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">
              <Link href="/reports" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
                <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Reports
              </Link>
              <span>/</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 font-medium">Generate Report</span>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden mb-6">
              <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-[#48a43f]/10 text-[#48a43f] flex items-center justify-center">
                  <FileSpreadsheet className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Export QA Report</h1>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Filter and download structured CSV test data</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="p-6">
                <form onSubmit={generate} className="space-y-5">
                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="report-type" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Report Dataset <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <select
                      id="report-type"
                      value={type}
                      onChange={(event) => setType(event.target.value)}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                    >
                      {reportTypes.map((item) => (
                        <option key={item.value} value={item.value}>
                          {item.label}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="report-project" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Project Filter
                    </label>
                    <select
                      id="report-project"
                      value={projectId}
                      onChange={(event) => setProjectId(event.target.value)}
                      disabled={loadingProjects}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                    >
                      <option value="">All accessible projects</option>
                      {projects.map((project) => (
                        <option key={project.id} value={project.id}>
                          {project.key} — {project.name}
                        </option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>

                  {error && (
                    <div role="alert" className="p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs font-medium">
                      {error}
                    </div>
                  )}

                  {message && (
                    <div role="status" className="p-3 bg-[#e8f5e9] text-[#2e7d32] border border-[#c8e6c9] rounded text-xs font-medium flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Download className="w-4 h-4" />
                      <span>{message}</span>
                    </div>
                  )}

                  <div className="pt-4 border-t border-[#edf2f7] flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={generating || loadingProjects}
                      className="btn-testrail-primary"
                    >
                      {generating ? 'Generating...' : 'Download CSV Report'}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => router.push('/reports')}
                      disabled={generating}
                      className="btn-testrail-secondary"
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>

                {rows.length > 0 && (
                  <section className="mt-8 pt-6 border-t border-[#edf2f7]">
                    <h2 className="text-xs font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-3">
                      Data Preview (First {rows.length} rows)
                    </h2>
                    <div className="overflow-x-auto border border-[#d8dee4] rounded">
                      <table className="w-full text-left">
                        <thead className="bg-[#f8fafc] border-b border-[#e2e8f0]">
                          <tr>
                            {columns.map((column) => (
                              <th key={column} className="px-3 py-2 text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">
                                {column}
                              </th>
                            ))}
                          </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#edf2f7]">
                          {rows.map((row, index) => (
                            <tr key={index} className="hover:bg-[#f8fafc]">
                              {columns.map((column) => (
                                <td key={column} className="px-3 py-2 text-xs text-slate-700 max-w-xs truncate">
                                  {row[column] == null ? '' : typeof row[column] === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(row[column]) : String(row[column])}
                                </td>
                              ))}
                            </tr>
                          ))}
                        </tbody>
                      </table>
                    </div>
                  </section>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\requirements\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { Plus, FileText } from 'lucide-react';

type Requirement = { id: string; req_key: string; title: string; project_id: string; type: string; priority: string; status: string };
type Project = { id: string; name: string };

export default function RequirementsPage() {
  const [requirements, setRequirements] = useState<Requirement[]>([]);
  const [projectNames, setProjectNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([fetch('/api/requirements', { cache: 'no-store' }), fetch('/api/projects', { cache: 'no-store' })])
      .then(async ([requirementsResponse, projectsResponse]) => {
        const [requirementsData, projectsData] = await Promise.all([requirementsResponse.json(), projectsResponse.json()]);
        if (!requirementsResponse.ok) throw new Error(requirementsData.error || 'Could not load requirements.');
        if (!projectsResponse.ok) throw new Error(projectsData.error || 'Could not load projects.');
        setRequirements(requirementsData.requirements ?? []);
        setProjectNames(Object.fromEntries((projectsData.projects as Project[]).map((project) => [project.id, project.name])));
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load requirements.'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const getStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status?.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'approved':
      case 'verified':
        return 'bg-[#dff0d8] dark:bg-[#132c1c] text-[#3c763d] dark:text-[#52c41a] border border-[#d6e9c6] dark:border-[#1d4c2b]';
      case 'under_review':
        return 'bg-[#fcf8e3] dark:bg-[#332711] text-[#8a6d3b] dark:text-[#faad14] border border-[#faebcc] dark:border-[#534019]';
      case 'implemented':
        return 'bg-[#e0f2fe] dark:bg-[#112d44] text-[#0369a1] dark:text-[#38bdf8] border border-[#bae6fd] dark:border-[#1e4d6d]';
      case 'rejected':
        return 'bg-[#f2dede] dark:bg-[#34161a] text-[#a94442] dark:text-[#ff7875] border border-[#ebccd1] dark:border-[#521e24]';
      case 'draft':
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700';
    }
  };

  const getPriorityBadge = (priority: string) => {
    switch (priority?.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'critical':
        return 'bg-[#f2dede] dark:bg-[#34161a] text-[#a94442] dark:text-[#ff7875] border border-[#ebccd1] dark:border-[#521e24]';
      case 'high':
        return 'bg-[#fcf8e3] dark:bg-[#332711] text-[#8a6d3b] dark:text-[#faad14] border border-[#faebcc] dark:border-[#534019]';
      case 'medium':
        return 'bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/40 text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-400 border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-900';
      case 'low':
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9] dark:bg-[#101622] text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 transition-colors">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {/* Header row */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">Requirements</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                Define and track functional, security, and performance specifications
              </p>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/requirements/new"
              className="bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 focus:outline-none"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.5]" />
              <span>Add Requirement</span>
            </Link>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900 rounded text-xs font-medium">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {loading ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 shadow-sm">
              Loading requirements...
            </div>
          ) : requirements.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-10 text-center shadow-sm">
              <FileText className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">No requirements found</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                Add requirements to link them directly to test cases and track test coverage.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/requirements/new"
                className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />
                Add your first requirement
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full min-w-[720px] text-left">
                  <thead className="bg-[#f8fafc] dark:bg-[#141d28] border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Key</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Title</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Project</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Type</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Priority</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Status</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#edf2f7] dark:divide-[#222e3e]">
                    {requirements.map((item) => (
                      <tr key={item.id} className="hover:bg-[#f8fafc] dark:hover:bg-[#1f2c3f] transition-colors">
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className="font-mono font-bold text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] bg-[#eef5fc] dark:bg-[#172739] border border-[#d2e3f5] dark:border-[#233f5d] px-2 py-0.5 rounded cursor-pointer hover:underline">
                            {item.req_key}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                          {item.title}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                          {projectNames[item.project_id] || 'General'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 capitalize">
                          {item.type.replace('_', ' ')}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className={`inline-block text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize ${getPriorityBadge(item.priority)}`}>
                            {item.priority}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className={`inline-block text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize ${getStatusBadge(item.status)}`}>
                            {item.status.replace('_', ' ')}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\requirements\new\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { ProjectSelector, type ProjectOption } from '@/components/forms/project-selector';
import { ArrowLeft, FileText } from 'lucide-react';

export default function NewRequirementPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [project, setProject] = useState<ProjectOption | null>(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ reqKey: '', title: '', description: '', type: 'functional', priority: 'medium', status: 'draft' });
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const submit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!project) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/requirements', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...form, projectId: project.id }),
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'Could not create the requirement.');
      router.push('/requirements');
    } catch (submitError: unknown) {
      setError(submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : 'Could not create the requirement.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            {/* Breadcrumb row */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">
              <Link href="/requirements" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
                <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Requirements
              </Link>
              <span>/</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 font-medium">Add Requirement</span>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden mb-6">
              <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-[#276fbf]/10 text-[#276fbf] flex items-center justify-center">
                  <FileText className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Add Requirement</h1>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Record a functional or technical requirement specification</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="p-6">
                {error && (
                  <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs font-medium">
                    {error}
                  </div>
                )}

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Target Project <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <ProjectSelector value={project?.id ?? ''} onChange={setProject} />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div className="sm:col-span-1">
                      <label htmlFor="req-key" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Requirement Key <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="req-key"
                        value={form.reqKey}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, reqKey: event.target.value.toUpperCase() })}
                        placeholder="e.g. REQ-101"
                        required
                        maxLength={30}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm font-mono text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                      <label htmlFor="req-title" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Title <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="req-title"
                        value={form.title}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, title: event.target.value })}
                        placeholder="e.g. User Authentication via Two-Factor OTP"
                        required
                        maxLength={255}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="req-description" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Description &amp; Acceptance Criteria
                    </label>
                    <textarea
                      id="req-description"
                      value={form.description}
                      onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, description: event.target.value })}
                      rows={4}
                      placeholder="Detailed requirement scope, acceptance criteria, and edge cases"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="req-type" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Type
                      </label>
                      <select
                        id="req-type"
                        value={form.type}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, type: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      >
                        <option value="functional">Functional</option>
                        <option value="non_functional">Non-functional</option>
                        <option value="business">Business</option>
                        <option value="performance">Performance</option>
                        <option value="security">Security</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="req-priority" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Priority
                      </label>
                      <select
                        id="req-priority"
                        value={form.priority}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, priority: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      >
                        <option value="low">Low</option>
                        <option value="medium">Medium</option>
                        <option value="high">High</option>
                        <option value="critical">Critical</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="req-status" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Status
                      </label>
                      <select
                        id="req-status"
                        value={form.status}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, status: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      >
                        <option value="draft">Draft</option>
                        <option value="under_review">Under review</option>
                        <option value="approved">Approved</option>
                        <option value="implemented">Implemented</option>
                        <option value="verified">Verified</option>
                        <option value="rejected">Rejected</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-4 border-t border-[#edf2f7] flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={saving || !project}
                      className="btn-testrail-primary"
                    >
                      {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Add Requirement'}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => router.push('/requirements')}
                      disabled={saving}
                      className="btn-testrail-secondary"
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\settings\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useRef, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { Building2, UserPlus, Users, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

type OrganizationMembership = {
  role: string;
  organization: { id: string; name: string; slug: string } | null;
};

type OrganizationMember = {
  userId: string;
  email: string;
  fullName: string;
  role: string;
  isCurrentUser: boolean;
};

async function fetchOrganizationMembers(organizationId: string): Promise<OrganizationMember[]> {
  const response = await fetch(`/api/organizations/${organizationId}`, { cache: 'no-store' });
  const data = await response.json();
  if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not load organization members.');
  return data.members ?? [];
}

export default function SettingsPage() {
  const [memberships, setMemberships] = useState<OrganizationMembership[]>([]);
  const [selectedOrgId, setSelectedOrgId] = useState('');
  const [orgName, setOrgName] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [organizationLoadError, setOrganizationLoadError] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState(false);
  const [settingsError, setSettingsError] = useState('');
  const [memberEmail, setMemberEmail] = useState('');
  const [memberRole, setMemberRole] = useState('qa_engineer');
  const [addingMember, setAddingMember] = useState(false);
  const [memberMessage, setMemberMessage] = useState('');
  const [memberError, setMemberError] = useState('');
  const [members, setMembers] = useState<OrganizationMember[]>([]);
  const [membersLoading, setMembersLoading] = useState(false);
  const [membersError, setMembersError] = useState('');
  const [memberToDeactivate, setMemberToDeactivate] = useState<OrganizationMember | null>(null);
  const [deactivatingUserId, setDeactivatingUserId] = useState('');
  const [deactivationError, setDeactivationError] = useState('');
  const [deactivationMessage, setDeactivationMessage] = useState('');
  const deactivationDialogRef = useRef<HTMLDialogElement>(null);

  const selectedMembership = memberships.find((item) => item.organization?.id === selectedOrgId);
  const org = selectedMembership?.organization ?? null;
  const canManageMembers = selectedMembership?.role === 'org_admin' || selectedMembership?.role === 'super_admin';

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetch('/api/organizations', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not load organization settings.');
        return data;
      })
      .then((data) => {
        if (!active) return;
        const available = (data.organizations ?? []).filter(
          (item: OrganizationMembership) => item.organization
        ) as OrganizationMembership[];
        setMemberships(available);
        const first = available[0];
        if (first?.organization) {
          setSelectedOrgId(first.organization.id);
          setOrgName(first.organization.name);
        }
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => {
        if (active) setOrganizationLoadError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load organization settings.');
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedOrgId || !canManageMembers) {
      setMembers([]);
      setMembersError('');
      return;
    }

    let active = true;
    setMembersLoading(true);
    setMembersError('');
    fetchOrganizationMembers(selectedOrgId)
      .then((items) => { if (active) setMembers(items); })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => {
        if (active) setMembersError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load organization members.');
      })
      .finally(() => { if (active) setMembersLoading(false); });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [selectedOrgId, canManageMembers]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const dialog = deactivationDialogRef.current;
    if (!dialog) return;
    if (memberToDeactivate && !dialog.open) dialog.showModal();
    if (!memberToDeactivate && dialog.open) dialog.close();
  }, [memberToDeactivate]);

  const handleOrganizationChange = (organizationId: string) => {
    setSelectedOrgId(organizationId);
    const next = memberships.find((item) => item.organization?.id === organizationId)?.organization;
    setOrgName(next?.name ?? '');
    setSaved(false);
    setSettingsError('');
    setMemberMessage('');
    setMemberError('');
    setDeactivationMessage('');
    setDeactivationError('');
    setMemberToDeactivate(null);
  };

  const handleSave = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!org) return;

    setSaving(true);
    setSaved(false);
    setSettingsError('');

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/organizations/${org.id}`, {
        method: 'PATCH',
        body: JSON.stringify({ name: orgName }),
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed to save organization settings.');

      setMemberships((current) => current.map((item) => item.organization?.id === org.id
        ? { ...item, organization: { ...item.organization, name: orgName } }
        : item
      ));
      setSaved(true);
    } catch (saveError: unknown) {
      setSettingsError(saveError instanceof Error ? saveError.message : 'Failed to save organization settings.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  const handleAddMember = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!org) return;

    setAddingMember(true);
    setMemberError('');
    setMemberMessage('');

    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/organizations/${org.id}`, {
        method: 'POST',
        body: JSON.stringify({ email: memberEmail, role: memberRole }),
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not add this member.');

      setMemberMessage(`${memberEmail.trim()} can now access ${org.name}.`);
      setMemberEmail('');
      setMembers(await fetchOrganizationMembers(org.id));
    } catch (addError: unknown) {
      setMemberError(addError instanceof Error ? addError.message : 'Could not add this member.');
    } finally {
      setAddingMember(false);
    }
  };

  const handleDeactivate = (member: OrganizationMember) => {
    setDeactivationError('');
    setDeactivationMessage('');
    setMemberToDeactivate(member);
  };

  const confirmDeactivate = async () => {
    const member = memberToDeactivate;
    if (!org || !member) return;

    setDeactivatingUserId(member.userId);
    setDeactivationError('');
    setDeactivationMessage('');
    try {
      const response = await fetch(`/api/organizations/${org.id}`, {
        method: 'DELETE',
        body: JSON.stringify({ userId: member.userId }),
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not deactivate this user.');

      setMembers((current) => current.filter((item) => item.userId !== member.userId));
      setDeactivationMessage(`${member.email} no longer has access to ${org.name}.`);
      setMemberToDeactivate(null);
    } catch (error: unknown) {
      setDeactivationError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : 'Could not deactivate this user.');
    } finally {
      setDeactivatingUserId('');
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-3xl space-y-6">
            <div className="pb-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0]">
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Organization Settings</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Manage your team workspace profile, permissions, and members</p>
            </div>

            {loading ? (
              <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 shadow-sm">
                Loading settings...
              </div>
            ) : organizationLoadError ? (
              <div role="alert" className="p-4 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded-md text-xs font-medium">
                <p>{organizationLoadError}</p>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => window.location.reload()}
                  className="mt-3 btn-testrail-secondary text-xs"
                >
                  Try again
                </button>
              </div>
            ) : memberships.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 shadow-sm">
                No organization found. Create an organization before managing settings.
              </div>
            ) : (
              <>
                {memberships.length > 1 && (
                  <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md p-4 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between gap-4">
                    <label htmlFor="settings-organization" className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 shrink-0">
                      Active Organization:
                    </label>
                    <select
                      id="settings-organization"
                      value={selectedOrgId}
                      onChange={(event) => handleOrganizationChange(event.target.value)}
                      className="w-full max-w-md px-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                    >
                      {memberships.map((item) => item.organization && (
                        <option key={item.organization.id} value={item.organization.id}>{item.organization.name}</option>
                      ))}
                    </select>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Organization Details Form */}
                <form onSubmit={handleSave} className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
                  <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Building2 className="w-4 h-4 text-[#276fbf]" />
                    <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider">Organization Profile</h2>
                  </div>

                  <div className="p-6 space-y-5">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="organization-name" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Organization Name <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="organization-name"
                        type="text"
                        value={orgName}
                        onChange={(event) => { setOrgName(event.target.value); setSaved(false); }}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                        required
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Subscription Plan</label>
                      <div className="p-4 bg-[#f8fafc] border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md flex items-center justify-between">
                        <div>
                          <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900">Enterprise QA Tier</p>
                          <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 mt-0.5">Unlimited test repositories, suites, executions, and AI assistant access</p>
                        </div>
                        <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 bg-[#dff0d8] text-[#3c763d] border border-[#d6e9c6] rounded-full">
                          Active License
                        </span>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {settingsError && <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-red-600 font-medium">{settingsError}</p>}
                    {saved && (
                      <p role="status" className="text-xs text-[#2e7d32] font-semibold flex items-center gap-1.5">
                        <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4" /> Settings saved successfully.
                      </p>
                    )}

                    <div className="pt-2">
                      <button
                        type="submit"
                        disabled={saving || !orgName.trim()}
                        className="btn-testrail-primary"
                      >
                        {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Save Changes'}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </form>

                {/* Team Members Management */}
                {canManageMembers ? (
                  <section className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
                    <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex items-center gap-2">
                      <Users className="w-4 h-4 text-[#48a43f]" />
                      <h2 className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900 uppercase tracking-wider">Team &amp; Access Control</h2>

                        ) : (
                          <div className="border border-[#d8dee4] rounded overflow-hidden">

                              {members.map((member) => (
                                <li key={member.userId} className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3 hover:bg-[#f8fafc] transition-colors">
                                  <div className="min-w-0">
                                    <p className="text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 truncate">{member.fullName}</p>
                                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500 truncate">{member.email}</p>
                                  </div>
                                  <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                                    <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200">
                                      {member.role.replaceAll('_', ' ')}
                                    </span>
                                    {member.isCurrentUser ? (
                                      <span className="text-[11px] font-semibold text-[#276fbf]">You</span>
                                    ) : member.role === 'org_admin' || member.role === 'super_admin' ? (
                                      <span className="text-[10px] text-slate-400 italic">Admin protected</span>
                                    ) : (
                                      <button
                                        type="button"
                                        onClick={() => handleDeactivate(member)}
                                        disabled={deactivatingUserId === member.userId}
                                        className="text-[11px] text-red-600 hover:text-red-800 font-medium hover:underline disabled:opacity-50"

                                        {deactivatingUserId === member.userId ? 'Deactivating...' : 'Deactivate'}
                                      </button>

                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 italic">Only an organization admin can add or manage team members.</p>
                )}
              </>
            )}
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>

      {/* Deactivation Dialog */}
      <dialog
        ref={deactivationDialogRef}
        aria-labelledby="deactivation-dialog-title"
        onCancel={(event) => {
          event.preventDefault();
          if (!deactivatingUserId) {
            setDeactivationError('');
            setMemberToDeactivate(null);
          }
        }}
        className="w-[calc(100%-2rem)] max-w-md rounded-md border border-[#d8dee4] bg-white p-6 text-slate-900 shadow-2xl backdrop:bg-black/50"
      >
        {memberToDeactivate && (
          <div>
            <h2 id="deactivation-dialog-title" className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Remove Member Access?</h2>
            <p className="mt-2 text-xs text-slate-600 leading-relaxed">
              <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{memberToDeactivate.fullName || memberToDeactivate.email}</span> ({memberToDeactivate.email}) will no longer be able to access <span className="font-semibold text-slate-900">{org?.name}</span>.
            </p>
            {deactivationError && <p role="alert" className="mt-3 text-xs text-red-600 font-medium">{deactivationError}</p>}
            <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-[#edf2f7]">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => { setDeactivationError(''); setMemberToDeactivate(null); }}
                disabled={deactivatingUserId === memberToDeactivate.userId}
                className="btn-testrail-secondary text-xs"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => void confirmDeactivate()}
                disabled={deactivatingUserId === memberToDeactivate.userId}
                className="bg-red-600 hover:bg-red-700 text-white text-xs font-semibold px-4 py-2 rounded shadow-sm disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {deactivatingUserId === memberToDeactivate.userId ? 'Deactivating...' : 'Deactivate Member'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </dialog>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { Plus, CheckSquare } from 'lucide-react';

type TestCase = { id: string; case_key: string; title: string; project_id: string; test_type: string; priority: string; automation_status: string };
type Project = { id: string; name: string };

export default function TestCasesPage() {
  const [testCases, setTestCases] = useState<TestCase[]>([]);
  const [projectNames, setProjectNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([fetch('/api/test-cases', { cache: 'no-store' }), fetch('/api/projects', { cache: 'no-store' })])
      .then(async ([casesResponse, projectsResponse]) => {
        const [casesData, projectsData] = await Promise.all([casesResponse.json(), projectsResponse.json()]);
        if (!casesResponse.ok) throw new Error(casesData.error || 'Could not load test cases.');
        if (!projectsResponse.ok) throw new Error(projectsData.error || 'Could not load projects.');
        setTestCases(casesData.testCases ?? []);
        setProjectNames(Object.fromEntries((projectsData.projects as Project[]).map((project) => [project.id, project.name])));
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load test cases.'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const getPriorityBadge = (priority: string) => {
    switch (priority?.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'critical':
        return 'bg-[#f2dede] dark:bg-[#34161a] text-[#a94442] dark:text-[#ff7875] border border-[#ebccd1] dark:border-[#521e24]';
      case 'high':
        return 'bg-[#fcf8e3] dark:bg-[#332711] text-[#8a6d3b] dark:text-[#faad14] border border-[#faebcc] dark:border-[#534019]';
      case 'medium':
        return 'bg-blue-50 dark:bg-blue-950/40 text-blue-700 dark:text-blue-400 border border-blue-200 dark:border-blue-900';
      case 'low':
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9] dark:bg-[#101622] text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 transition-colors">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {/* Header row */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">Test Cases</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                Test case repository for manual and automated test execution
              </p>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/test-cases/new"
              className="bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 focus:outline-none"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.5]" />
              <span>Add Test Case</span>
            </Link>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900 rounded text-xs font-medium">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {loading ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 shadow-sm">
              Loading test cases...
            </div>
          ) : testCases.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-10 text-center shadow-sm">
              <CheckSquare className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">No test cases yet</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                Create structured test cases with preconditions, test types, and priorities.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/test-cases/new"
                className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />
                Add your first test case
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full min-w-[720px] text-left">
                  <thead className="bg-[#f8fafc] dark:bg-[#141d28] border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Key</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Title</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Project</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Type</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Priority</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Automation</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#edf2f7] dark:divide-[#222e3e]">
                    {testCases.map((item) => (
                      <tr key={item.id} className="hover:bg-[#f8fafc] dark:hover:bg-[#1f2c3f] transition-colors">
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className="font-mono font-bold text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] bg-[#eef5fc] dark:bg-[#172739] border border-[#d2e3f5] dark:border-[#233f5d] px-2 py-0.5 rounded cursor-pointer hover:underline">
                            {item.case_key}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs font-medium text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                          {item.title}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400">
                          {projectNames[item.project_id] || 'General'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 capitalize">
                          {item.test_type}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className={`inline-block text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize ${getPriorityBadge(item.priority)}`}>
                            {item.priority}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className={`inline-block text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize ${
                            item.automation_status === 'automated'
                              ? 'bg-[#dff0d8] dark:bg-[#132c1c] text-[#3c763d] dark:text-[#52c41a] border border-[#d6e9c6] dark:border-[#1d4c2b]'
                              : 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700'
                          }`}>
                            {item.automation_status.replace('_', ' ')}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\test-cases\new\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { ProjectSelector, type ProjectOption } from '@/components/forms/project-selector';
import { ArrowLeft, CheckSquare } from 'lucide-react';

type RequirementOption = { id: string; req_key: string; title: string };

export default function NewTestCasePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [project, setProject] = useState<ProjectOption | null>(null);
  const [requirements, setRequirements] = useState<RequirementOption[]>([]);
  const [requirementIds, setRequirementIds] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [loadingRequirements, setLoadingRequirements] = useState(false);
  const [requirementsError, setRequirementsError] = useState('');
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ caseKey: '', title: '', description: '', preconditions: '', testType: 'functional', priority: 'medium', category: 'General', automationStatus: 'manual' });
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setRequirements([]);
    setRequirementIds([]);
    setRequirementsError('');
    setLoadingRequirements(false);

    if (!project?.id) return () => { active = false; };

    setLoadingRequirements(true);
    fetch(`/api/requirements?projectId=${encodeURIComponent(project.id)}`, { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not load project requirements.');
        return data as { requirements: RequirementOption[] };
      })
      .then((data) => {
        if (active) setRequirements(data.requirements ?? []);
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => {
        if (active) setRequirementsError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load project requirements.');
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoadingRequirements(false);
      });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [project?.id]);

  const submit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!project) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/test-cases', {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...form, projectId: project.id, requirementIds }),
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'Could not create the test case.');
      router.push('/test-cases');
    } catch (submitError: unknown) {
      setError(submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : 'Could not create the test case.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-3xl mx-auto">
            {/* Breadcrumb row */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">
              <Link href="/test-cases" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
                <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Test Cases
              </Link>
              <span>/</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 font-medium">Add Test Case</span>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden mb-6">
              <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-[#48a43f]/10 text-[#48a43f] flex items-center justify-center">
                  <CheckSquare className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Add Test Case</h1>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Define test steps, preconditions, type, and priorities</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="p-6">
                {error && (
                  <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs font-medium">
                    {error}
                  </div>
                )}

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Project <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <ProjectSelector value={project?.id ?? ''} onChange={setProject} />
                  </div>

                  <fieldset className="p-3.5 rounded border border-[#d8dee4] bg-[#f8fafc]/50 space-y-2">
                    <legend className="text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 px-1">
                      Linked Requirements <span className="font-normal text-slate-400 lowercase">(optional)</span>
                    </legend>
                    {!project ? (
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Select a project to see its requirements.</p>
                    ) : loadingRequirements ? (
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">Loading requirements…</p>
                    ) : requirementsError ? (
                      <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-red-500 font-medium">{requirementsError}</p>
                    ) : requirements.length ? (
                      <div className="max-h-40 space-y-2 overflow-y-auto rounded border border-[#d8dee4] bg-white p-2.5">
                        {requirements.map((requirement) => (
                          <label key={requirement.id} className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-2.5 text-xs text-slate-700 hover:text-slate-900">
                            <input
                              type="checkbox"
                              checked={requirementIds.includes(requirement.id)}
                              onChange={(event) => setRequirementIds((selected) => event.target.checked
                                ? [...selected, requirement.id]
                                : selected.filter((id) => id !== requirement.id))}
                              className="mt-0.5 accent-[#48a43f]"
                            />
                            <span>
                              <span className="mr-1.5 font-mono font-bold text-[#276fbf]">{requirement.req_key}</span>
                              {requirement.title}
                            </span>
                          </label>
                        ))}
                      </div>
                    ) : (
                      <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">This project has no requirements to link yet.</p>
                    )}
                  </fieldset>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">
                    <div className="sm:col-span-1">
                      <label htmlFor="case-key" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Case Key <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="case-key"
                        value={form.caseKey}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, caseKey: event.target.value.toUpperCase() })}
                        placeholder="e.g. TC-101"
                        required
                        maxLength={30}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm font-mono text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                      />
                    </div>
                    <div className="sm:col-span-2">
                      <label htmlFor="case-title" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Title <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="case-title"
                        value={form.title}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, title: event.target.value })}
                        placeholder="e.g. User login with valid credentials"
                        required
                        maxLength={255}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="case-preconditions" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Preconditions
                    </label>
                    <textarea
                      id="case-preconditions"
                      value={form.preconditions}
                      onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, preconditions: event.target.value })}
                      placeholder="e.g. User account exists in database and email is verified"
                      rows={2}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="case-description" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Steps & Expected Result
                    </label>
                    <textarea
                      id="case-description"
                      value={form.description}
                      onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, description: event.target.value })}
                      placeholder="Step 1: Enter email...&#10;Step 2: Click submit...&#10;Expected Result: Dashboard is displayed"
                      rows={4}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-4 gap-4">
                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="case-type" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Type</label>
                      <select
                        id="case-type"
                        value={form.testType}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, testType: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      >
                        <option value="functional">Functional</option>
                        <option value="regression">Regression</option>
                        <option value="integration">Integration</option>
                        <option value="system">System</option>
                        <option value="api">API</option>
                        <option value="ui">UI</option>
                        <option value="performance">Performance</option>
                        <option value="security">Security</option>
                        <option value="exploratory">Exploratory</option>
                        <option value="uat">UAT</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="case-priority" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Priority</label>
                      <select
                        id="case-priority"
                        value={form.priority}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, priority: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      >
                        <option value="low">Low</option>
                        <option value="medium">Medium</option>
                        <option value="high">High</option>
                        <option value="critical">Critical</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="case-category" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Section / Category</label>
                      <input
                        id="case-category"
                        value={form.category}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, category: event.target.value })}
                        maxLength={100}
                        placeholder="General"
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="case-automation" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">Automation</label>
                      <select
                        id="case-automation"
                        value={form.automationStatus}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, automationStatus: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      >
                        <option value="manual">Manual</option>
                        <option value="automated">Automated</option>
                        <option value="to_automate">To automate</option>
                        <option value="not_automatable">Not automatable</option>
                      </select>

                      className="btn-testrail-primary"

                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => router.push('/test-cases')}
                      disabled={saving}
                      className="btn-testrail-secondary"
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>

            </div>
          </div>
