===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\test-plans\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { Plus, Calendar } from 'lucide-react';

type TestPlan = { id: string; title: string; project_id: string; scope: string | null; status: string; start_date: string | null; end_date: string | null };
type Project = { id: string; name: string };

export default function TestPlansPage() {
  const [plans, setPlans] = useState<TestPlan[]>([]);
  const [projectNames, setProjectNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([fetch('/api/test-plans', { cache: 'no-store' }), fetch('/api/projects', { cache: 'no-store' })])
      .then(async ([plansResponse, projectsResponse]) => {
        const [plansData, projectsData] = await Promise.all([plansResponse.json(), projectsResponse.json()]);
        if (!plansResponse.ok) throw new Error(plansData.error || 'Could not load test plans.');
        if (!projectsResponse.ok) throw new Error(projectsData.error || 'Could not load projects.');
        setPlans(plansData.testPlans ?? []);
        setProjectNames(Object.fromEntries((projectsData.projects as Project[]).map((project) => [project.id, project.name])));
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load test plans.'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const getStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status?.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'completed':
        return 'bg-[#dff0d8] dark:bg-[#132c1c] text-[#3c763d] dark:text-[#52c41a] border border-[#d6e9c6] dark:border-[#1d4c2b]';
      case 'in_progress':
        return 'bg-[#e0f2fe] dark:bg-[#112d44] text-[#0369a1] dark:text-[#38bdf8] border border-[#bae6fd] dark:border-[#1e4d6d]';
      case 'planning':
      default:
        return 'bg-amber-50 dark:bg-amber-950/40 text-amber-700 dark:text-amber-400 border border-amber-200 dark:border-amber-900';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9] dark:bg-[#101622] text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 transition-colors">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {/* Header row */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">Test Plans</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                Organize releases, milestones, test suites, and schedule testing cycles
              </p>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/test-plans/new"
              className="bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 focus:outline-none"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.5]" />
              <span>Add Test Plan</span>
            </Link>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900 rounded text-xs font-medium">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {loading ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 shadow-sm">
              Loading test plans...
            </div>
          ) : plans.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-10 text-center shadow-sm">
              <Calendar className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">No test plans yet</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                Group multiple test suites and test runs together for a release or sprint milestone.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/test-plans/new"
                className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />
                Create your first test plan
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-2 gap-5">
              {plans.map((plan) => (
                <section
                  key={plan.id}
                  className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-5 shadow-sm hover:shadow hover:border-[#b0c4de] dark:hover:border-[#3a4e6b] transition-all flex flex-col justify-between"
                >
                  <div>
                    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-3 mb-2">
                      <div>
                        <h2 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">
                          {plan.title}
                        </h2>
                        <p className="text-xs font-medium text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] mt-0.5">
                          {projectNames[plan.project_id] || 'General Project'}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                      <span className={`text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize shrink-0 ${getStatusBadge(plan.status)}`}>
                        {plan.status.replace('_', ' ')}
                      </span>
                    </div>

                    {plan.scope && (
                      <p className="mt-3 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 line-clamp-3 leading-relaxed bg-[#f8fafc] dark:bg-[#141d28] p-2.5 rounded border border-[#edf2f7] dark:border-[#222e3e]">
                        {plan.scope}
                      </p>
                    )}
                  </div>

                  <div className="mt-4 pt-3 border-t border-[#edf2f7] dark:border-[#222e3e] flex items-center justify-between text-[11px] text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500">
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-1">
                      <Calendar className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400 dark:text-slate-500" />
                      {plan.start_date || 'No start date'}
                      {plan.end_date ? ` — ${plan.end_date}` : ''}
                    </span>
                    <span className="font-mono text-[10px]">ID: {plan.id.slice(0, 8)}</span>
                  </div>
                </section>
              ))}
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\test-plans\new\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { ProjectSelector, type ProjectOption } from '@/components/forms/project-selector';
import { ArrowLeft, Calendar } from 'lucide-react';

export default function NewTestPlanPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [project, setProject] = useState<ProjectOption | null>(null);
  const [form, setForm] = useState({ title: '', scope: '', startDate: '', endDate: '', status: 'planning' });
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const submit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!project) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/test-plans', {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ...form, projectId: project.id }),
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'Could not create the test plan.');
      router.push('/test-plans');
    } catch (submitError: unknown) {
      setError(submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : 'Could not create the test plan.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            {/* Breadcrumb row */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">
              <Link href="/test-plans" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
                <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Test Plans
              </Link>
              <span>/</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 font-medium">Add Test Plan</span>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden mb-6">
              <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-[#276fbf]/10 text-[#276fbf] flex items-center justify-center">
                  <Calendar className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Add Test Plan</h1>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Group test suites for a milestone, release, or sprint</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="p-6">
                {error && (
                  <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs font-medium">
                    {error}
                  </div>
                )}

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Target Project <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <ProjectSelector value={project?.id ?? ''} onChange={setProject} />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="plan-title" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Test Plan Title <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="plan-title"
                      value={form.title}
                      onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, title: event.target.value })}
                      placeholder="e.g. Release 2.0 Acceptance Testing"
                      required
                      maxLength={255}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="plan-scope" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Scope &amp; Description
                    </label>
                    <textarea
                      id="plan-scope"
                      value={form.scope}
                      onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, scope: event.target.value })}
                      rows={4}
                      placeholder="Features, test areas, and release criteria included in this plan"
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                    />
                  </div>

                  <div className="grid grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3 gap-4">

                      <label htmlFor="plan-start" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Start Date
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="plan-start"
                        type="date"
                        value={form.startDate}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, startDate: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="plan-end" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        End Date
                      </label>
                      <input
                        id="plan-end"
                        type="date"
                        min={form.startDate || undefined}
                        value={form.endDate}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, endDate: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      />
                    </div>

                    <div>
                      <label htmlFor="plan-status" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                        Status
                      </label>
                      <select
                        id="plan-status"
                        value={form.status}
                        onChange={(event) => setForm({ ...form, status: event.target.value })}
                        className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-xs text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f]"
                      >
                        <option value="planning">Planning</option>
                        <option value="in_progress">In progress</option>
                        <option value="completed">Completed</option>
                      </select>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-4 border-t border-[#edf2f7] flex items-center gap-3">
                    <button
                      type="submit"
                      disabled={saving || !project}
                      className="btn-testrail-primary"
                    >
                      {saving ? 'Saving...' : 'Add Test Plan'}
                    </button>
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => router.push('/test-plans')}
                      disabled={saving}
                      className="btn-testrail-secondary"
                    >
                      Cancel
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </form>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { Plus, PlayCircle } from 'lucide-react';

type TestRun = { id: string; name: string; project_id: string; test_plan_id: string | null; status: string; started_at: string };
type Project = { id: string; name: string };

export default function TestRunsPage() {
  const [runs, setRuns] = useState<TestRun[]>([]);
  const [projectNames, setProjectNames] = useState<Record<string, string>>({});
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    Promise.all([fetch('/api/test-runs', { cache: 'no-store' }), fetch('/api/projects', { cache: 'no-store' })])
      .then(async ([runsResponse, projectsResponse]) => {
        const [runsData, projectsData] = await Promise.all([runsResponse.json(), projectsResponse.json()]);
        if (!runsResponse.ok) throw new Error(runsData.error || 'Could not load test runs.');
        if (!projectsResponse.ok) throw new Error(projectsData.error || 'Could not load projects.');
        setRuns(runsData.testRuns ?? []);
        setProjectNames(Object.fromEntries((projectsData.projects as Project[]).map((project) => [project.id, project.name])));
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load test runs.'))
      .finally(() => setLoading(false));
  }, []);

  const getStatusBadge = (status: string) => {
    switch (status?.toLowerCase()) {
      case 'completed':
        return 'bg-[#dff0d8] dark:bg-[#132c1c] text-[#3c763d] dark:text-[#52c41a] border border-[#d6e9c6] dark:border-[#1d4c2b]';
      case 'in_progress':
        return 'bg-[#e0f2fe] dark:bg-[#112d44] text-[#0369a1] dark:text-[#38bdf8] border border-[#bae6fd] dark:border-[#1e4d6d]';
      default:
        return 'bg-slate-100 dark:bg-slate-800 text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300 border border-slate-200 dark:border-slate-700';
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9] dark:bg-[#101622] text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-100 transition-colors">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {/* Header row */}
          <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-4 mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white tracking-tight">Test Runs &amp; Results</h1>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                Execute test suites, record pass/fail results, and monitor execution milestones
              </p>
            </div>
            <Link
              href="/test-runs/new"
              className="bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 focus:outline-none"
            >
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.5]" />
              <span>Add Test Run</span>
            </Link>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 dark:bg-red-950/40 text-red-700 dark:text-red-400 border border-red-200 dark:border-red-900 rounded text-xs font-medium">
              {error}
            </div>
          )}

          {loading ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 shadow-sm">
              Loading test runs...
            </div>
          ) : runs.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md p-10 text-center shadow-sm">
              <PlayCircle className="w-10 h-10 text-slate-300 dark:text-slate-600 mx-auto mb-3" />
              <h3 className="text-sm font-semibold text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200">No test runs found</h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 mt-1 max-w-sm mx-auto">
                Start a new test run to execute tests and log results against your project test cases.
              </p>
              <Link
                href="/test-runs/new"
                className="mt-4 inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3.5 py-2 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors"
              >
                <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />
                Start a test run
              </Link>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full min-w-[640px] text-left">
                  <thead className="bg-[#f8fafc] dark:bg-[#141d28] border-b border-[#e2e8f0] dark:border-[#28374d]">
                    <tr>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Run Name</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Project</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Status</th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Started</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#edf2f7] dark:divide-[#222e3e]">
                    {runs.map((run) => (
                      <tr key={run.id} className="hover:bg-[#f8fafc] dark:hover:bg-[#1f2c3f] transition-colors">
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs font-bold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">
                          <span className="text-[#276fbf] dark:text-[#4da3ff] hover:underline cursor-pointer">
                            {run.name}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-600 dark:text-slate-300">
                          {projectNames[run.project_id] || 'General Project'}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs">
                          <span className={`inline-block text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize ${getStatusBadge(run.status)}`}>
                            {run.status.replace('_', ' ')}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 font-mono">
                          {new Date(run.started_at).toLocaleString()}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </main>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState, type FormEvent } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { ProjectSelector, type ProjectOption } from '@/components/forms/project-selector';
import { ArrowLeft, PlayCircle } from 'lucide-react';

export default function NewTestRunPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [project, setProject] = useState<ProjectOption | null>(null);
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  const submit = async (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (!project) return;
    setSaving(true);
    setError('');
    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/test-runs', {
        method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ name, projectId: project.id }),
      });
      const result = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(result.error || 'Could not create the test run.');
      router.push('/test-runs');
    } catch (submitError: unknown) {
      setError(submitError instanceof Error ? submitError.message : 'Could not create the test run.');
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          <div className="max-w-2xl mx-auto">
            {/* Breadcrumb row */}
            <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-slate-500 mb-4">
              <Link href="/test-runs" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline flex items-center gap-1">
                <ArrowLeft className="w-3.5 h-3.5" /> Back to Test Runs
              </Link>
              <span>/</span>
              <span className="text-slate-800 font-medium">Add Test Run</span>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden mb-6">
              <div className="bg-[#f8fafc] px-6 py-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex items-center gap-2.5">
                <div className="w-7 h-7 rounded bg-[#48a43f]/10 text-[#48a43f] flex items-center justify-center">
                  <PlayCircle className="w-4 h-4" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h1 className="text-base font-bold text-slate-900">Start New Test Run</h1>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">Initialize test execution session across project test cases</p>
                </div>
              </div>

              <div className="p-6">
                {error && (
                  <div role="alert" className="mb-4 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs font-medium">
                    {error}
                  </div>
                )}

                <form onSubmit={submit} className="space-y-5">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Target Project <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <ProjectSelector value={project?.id ?? ''} onChange={setProject} />
                  </div>

                  <div>
                    <label htmlFor="run-name" className="block text-xs font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-700 mb-1.5">
                      Test Run Name <span className="text-red-500">*</span>
                    </label>
                    <input
                      id="run-name"
                      value={name}
                      onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
                      placeholder="e.g. Sprint 24 Release Regression"
                      required
                      maxLength={255}
                      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-slate-800 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f]"
                    />
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-1.5">
                      This run will include all test cases currently in the selected project, initialized as <span className="font-semibold text-slate-600">Untested</span>.
                    </p>
                  </div>

                  <div className="pt-4 border-t border-[#edf2f7] flex items-center gap-3">

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\app\traceability\page.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { Header } from '@/components/dashboard/header';
import { Sidebar } from '@/components/dashboard/sidebar';
import { GitFork, CheckCircle2, AlertTriangle, Layers, X } from 'lucide-react';

type TraceabilityTestCase = { id: string; key: string; title: string };
type TestCaseOption = { id: string; case_key: string; title: string };
type TraceabilityRow = {
  id: string;
  projectId: string;
  key: string;
  title: string;
  status: string;
  projectName: string;
  testCases: TraceabilityTestCase[];
  openDefects: number;
};
type TraceabilitySummary = {
  totalRequirements: number;
  coveredRequirements: number;
  coveragePercentage: number;
  openDefects: number;
};

const EMPTY_SUMMARY: TraceabilitySummary = {
  totalRequirements: 0,
  coveredRequirements: 0,
  coveragePercentage: 0,
  openDefects: 0,
};

export default function TraceabilityPage() {
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<TraceabilityRow[]>([]);
  const [summary, setSummary] = useState<TraceabilitySummary>(EMPTY_SUMMARY);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
  const [editingRequirementId, setEditingRequirementId] = useState('');
  const [editingProjectId, setEditingProjectId] = useState('');
  const [availableTestCases, setAvailableTestCases] = useState<TestCaseOption[]>([]);
  const [loadingTestCases, setLoadingTestCases] = useState(false);
  const [testCaseLoadError, setTestCaseLoadError] = useState('');
  const [linkError, setLinkError] = useState('');
  const [updatingCaseId, setUpdatingCaseId] = useState('');
  const editingRequirement = rows.find((row) => row.id === editingRequirementId) ?? null;

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setLoading(true);
    setError('');

    fetch('/api/traceability', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not load traceability data.');
        return data as { requirements: TraceabilityRow[]; summary: TraceabilitySummary };
      })
      .then((data) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setRows(data.requirements ?? []);
        setSummary(data.summary ?? EMPTY_SUMMARY);
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setRows([]);
        setSummary(EMPTY_SUMMARY);
        setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load traceability data.');
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [retry]);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setAvailableTestCases([]);
    setTestCaseLoadError('');
    if (!editingProjectId) return () => { active = false; };

    setLoadingTestCases(true);
    fetch(`/api/test-cases?projectId=${encodeURIComponent(editingProjectId)}`, { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not load test cases.');
        return data as { testCases: TestCaseOption[] };
      })
      .then((data) => {
        if (active) setAvailableTestCases(data.testCases ?? []);
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => {
        if (active) setTestCaseLoadError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load test cases.');
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (active) setLoadingTestCases(false);
      });

    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [editingProjectId]);

  const toggleTestCaseLink = async (testCaseId: string, checked: boolean) => {
    if (!editingRequirement) return;
    setUpdatingCaseId(testCaseId);
    setLinkError('');

    try {
      const response = await fetch('/api/traceability', {
        method: checked ? 'POST' : 'DELETE',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ requirementId: editingRequirement.id, testCaseId }),
      });
      const data = await response.json();
      if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not update this requirement link.');
      setRetry((value) => value + 1);
    } catch (linkUpdateError: unknown) {
      setLinkError(linkUpdateError instanceof Error ? linkUpdateError.message : 'Could not update this requirement link.');
    } finally {
      setUpdatingCaseId('');
    }
  };

  const closeLinkManager = () => {
    setEditingRequirementId('');
    setEditingProjectId('');
    setAvailableTestCases([]);
    setLinkError('');
  };

  return (
    <div className="flex h-screen bg-[#f5f7f9]">
      <Sidebar />
      <div className="flex-1 flex flex-col overflow-hidden">
        <Header />
        <main className="flex-1 overflow-y-auto p-6">
          {/* Header row */}
          <div className="mb-6 pb-4 border-b border-[#e2e8f0]">
            <h1 className="text-xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">Traceability Matrix</h1>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">
              Requirement-to-test coverage verification and open defect correlation
            </p>
          </div>

          {error && (
            <div role="alert" className="mb-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 p-3 bg-red-50 text-red-700 border border-red-200 rounded text-xs font-medium">
              <span>{error}</span>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setRetry((value) => value + 1)}
                className="btn-testrail-secondary text-xs py-1"
              >
                Retry
              </button>
            </div>
          )}

          {/* TestRail Summary Cards */}
          <section aria-label="Traceability summary" className="mb-6 grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
            <SummaryCard label="Total Requirements" value={loading ? '—' : summary.totalRequirements} icon={Layers} border="border-t-4 border-[#276fbf]" />
            <SummaryCard label="Covered Requirements" value={loading ? '—' : summary.coveredRequirements} icon={CheckCircle2} border="border-t-4 border-[#48a43f]" />
            <SummaryCard label="Test Coverage" value={loading ? '—' : `${summary.coveragePercentage}%`} icon={GitFork} border="border-t-4 border-indigo-500" />
            <SummaryCard label="Open Linked Defects" value={loading ? '—' : summary.openDefects} icon={AlertTriangle} border="border-t-4 border-amber-500" />
          </section>

          {/* Traceability Table */}
          <section className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-md shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
            <div className="overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="w-full min-w-[980px] text-left">
                <thead className="bg-[#f8fafc] border-b border-[#e2e8f0]">
                  <tr>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Requirement</th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Linked Test Cases</th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Coverage</th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Open Defects</th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider">Status</th>
                    <th scope="col" className="px-4 py-3 text-[11px] font-bold text-slate-500 uppercase tracking-wider text-right">Actions</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="divide-y divide-[#edf2f7]">
                  {loading ? (
                    <tr><td colSpan={6} className="p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">Loading traceability matrix…</td></tr>
                  ) : error ? (
                    <tr><td colSpan={6} className="p-8 text-center text-xs text-slate-500">Traceability data is unavailable. Retry after resolving the error above.</td></tr>
                  ) : rows.length ? rows.map((row) => (
                    <tr key={row.id} className="hover:bg-[#f8fafc] transition-colors">
                      <td className="px-4 py-3.5 align-top">
                        <span className="font-mono text-xs font-bold text-[#276fbf] bg-[#eef5fc] border border-[#d2e3f5] px-2 py-0.5 rounded">
                          {row.key}
                        </span>
                        <div className="mt-1.5 text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900">{row.title}</div>
                        <div className="mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-slate-500">{row.projectName}</div>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3.5 align-top">
                        {row.testCases.length ? (
                          <div className="space-y-1.5">
                            {row.testCases.map((testCase) => (
                              <div key={testCase.id} className="text-xs flex items-center gap-1.5">
                                <span className="rounded bg-slate-100 border border-slate-200 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[10px] font-bold text-slate-700">{testCase.key}</span>
                                <span className="text-slate-700 truncate max-w-xs">{testCase.title}</span>
                              </div>
                            ))}
                          </div>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">No test cases linked</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3.5 align-top">
                        {row.testCases.length ? (
                          <span className="bg-[#dff0d8] text-[#3c763d] border border-[#d6e9c6] font-semibold text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                            ? Covered
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="bg-[#fcf8e3] text-[#8a6d3b] border border-[#faebcc] font-semibold text-[10px] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full inline-flex items-center gap-1">
                            ? Uncovered
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3.5 align-top">
                        <span className={`text-xs font-bold ${row.openDefects ? 'text-red-600' : 'text-[#3c763d]'}`}>
                          {row.openDefects}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3.5 align-top">
                        <span className="inline-block text-[10px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full capitalize bg-slate-100 text-slate-700 border border-slate-200">
                          {row.status.replaceAll('_', ' ')}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3.5 align-top text-right">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          onClick={() => {
                            setEditingRequirementId(row.id);
                            setEditingProjectId(row.projectId);
                            setLinkError('');
                          }}
                          className="btn-testrail-secondary text-xs py-1 px-2.5"
                        >
                          Manage links
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )) : (
                    <tr>
                      <td colSpan={6} className="p-8 text-center">
                        <p className="text-xs text-slate-500">No requirements found for your organizations.</p>
                        <Link href="/requirements/new" className="mt-2 inline-block text-xs font-semibold text-[#276fbf] hover:underline">
                          + Create a requirement
                        </Link>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  )}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </section>
        </main>
      </div>

      {editingRequirement && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/50 p-4" role="presentation">
          <section role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby="link-dialog-title" className="w-full max-w-xl rounded-md border border-[#d8dee4] bg-white p-6 shadow-2xl">
            <div className="mb-4 pb-3 border-b border-[#e2e8f0] flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
              <div>
                <h2 id="link-dialog-title" className="text-sm font-bold text-slate-900">Link Test Cases to Requirement</h2>
                <p className="mt-0.5 text-xs text-slate-500">
                  <span className="font-mono font-bold text-[#276fbf]">{editingRequirement.key}:</span> {editingRequirement.title}
                </p>
              </div>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={closeLinkManager}
                className="rounded p-1 text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-100 hover:text-slate-700"
                aria-label="Close link manager"
              >
                <X className="w-4 h-4" />
              </button>
            </div>

            {linkError && <p role="alert" className="mb-3 text-xs text-red-600 font-medium">{linkError}</p>}
            {testCaseLoadError && <p role="alert" className="mb-3 text-xs text-red-600 font-medium">{testCaseLoadError}</p>}
            {loadingTestCases ? (
              <p className="py-4 text-xs text-slate-400 text-center">Loading project test cases…</p>
            ) : availableTestCases.length ? (
              <div className="max-h-72 space-y-1.5 overflow-y-auto rounded border border-[#d8dee4] p-3 bg-[#f8fafc]/50">
                {availableTestCases.map((testCase) => (
                  <label key={testCase.id} className="flex cursor-pointer items-start gap-2.5 rounded p-2 text-xs text-slate-700 hover:bg-white hover:shadow-sm border border-transparent hover:border-slate-200 transition-all">
                    <input
                      type="checkbox"
                      checked={editingRequirement.testCases.some((item) => item.id === testCase.id)}
                      disabled={!!updatingCaseId}
                      onChange={(event) => toggleTestCaseLink(testCase.id, event.target.checked)}
                      className="mt-0.5 accent-[#48a43f]"
                    />
                    <span>
                      <span className="mr-1.5 font-mono font-bold text-[#276fbf]">{testCase.case_key}</span>
                      {testCase.title}
                    </span>
                    {updatingCaseId === testCase.id && <span className="ml-auto text-[10px] text-slate-400">Saving…</span>}
                  </label>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : !testCaseLoadError ? (
              <p className="py-4 text-xs text-slate-400 text-center">This project has no test cases to link.</p>
            ) : null}

            <div className="mt-5 pt-3 border-t border-[#edf2f7] flex justify-end">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={closeLinkManager}
                className="btn-testrail-primary"
              >
                Done
              </button>
            </div>
          </section>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function SummaryCard({ label, value, icon: Icon, border }: { label: string; value: string | number; icon: any; border: string }) {
  return (
    <div className={`rounded-md border border-[#d8dee4] bg-white p-4 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between ${border}`}>
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
        <p className="text-[11px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-500">{label}</p>
        <Icon className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-400" />
      </div>
      <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-slate-900 tracking-tight">{value}</p>
    </div>
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\components\auth\AuthProvider.tsx =====
'use client';

import { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { Session, User } from '@supabase/supabase-js';

const AuthContext = createContext<{ user: User | null; session: Session | null; loading: boolean }>({
  user: null,
  session: null,
  loading: true,
});

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [user, setUser] = useState<User | null>(null);
  const [session, setSession] = useState<Session | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    let unsubscribe: (() => void) | undefined;

    void import('@/lib/supabase/client')
      .then(({ supabase }) => {
        if (!active) return;

        const { data: { subscription } } = supabase.auth.onAuthStateChange((_event, nextSession) => {
          setSession(nextSession);
          setUser(nextSession?.user ?? null);
        });
        unsubscribe = () => subscription.unsubscribe();

        return supabase.auth.getSession().then(({ data: { session: currentSession } }) => {
          if (!active) return;
          setSession(currentSession);
          setUser(currentSession?.user ?? null);
          setLoading(false);
        });
      })
      .catch((error: unknown) => {
        console.error('Failed to initialize the authentication session:', error);
        if (active) setLoading(false);
      });

    return () => {
      active = false;
      unsubscribe?.();
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <AuthContext.Provider value={{ user, session, loading }}>
      {children}
    </AuthContext.Provider>
  );
}

export const useAuth = () => useContext(AuthContext);



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\components\dashboard\header.tsx =====
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useAuth } from '@/components/auth/AuthProvider';
import { ThemeToggle } from '@/components/theme/ThemeToggle';
import { Plus, Building2, User, LogOut, CheckCircle2 } from 'lucide-react';

type OrganizationMembership = { organization: { name: string } | null };
type OrganizationLoadState = 'loading' | 'available' | 'empty' | 'error';

export function Header() {
  const { user } = useAuth();
  const router = useRouter();
  const [organizationName, setOrganizationName] = useState('');
  const [organizationLoadState, setOrganizationLoadState] = useState<OrganizationLoadState>('loading');
  const [organizationRetry, setOrganizationRetry] = useState(0);
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [signingOut, setSigningOut] = useState(false);
  const [signOutError, setSignOutError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    setOrganizationName('');
    setOrganizationLoadState('loading');

    if (!user?.id) {
      return () => { active = false; };
    }

    fetch('/api/organizations', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (response) => {
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Could not load organizations');
        return response.json();
      })
      .then((data) => {
        if (!active) return;
        if (!Array.isArray(data.organizations)) throw new Error('Organization response was invalid');
        const memberships = (data.organizations ?? []) as OrganizationMembership[];
        const name = memberships.find((item) => item.organization?.name)?.organization?.name || '';
        setOrganizationName(name);
        setOrganizationLoadState(name ? 'available' : 'empty');
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (active) setOrganizationLoadState('error');
      });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [user?.id, organizationRetry]);

  const handleSignOut = async () => {
    setSigningOut(true);
    setSignOutError('');
    try {
      const { supabase } = await import('@/lib/supabase/client');
      const { error } = await supabase.auth.signOut();
      if (error) {
        setSignOutError(error.message);
        return;
      }
      setMenuOpen(false);
      router.replace('/auth/login');
      router.refresh();
    } catch {
      setSignOutError('Could not sign out. Please try again.');
    } finally {
      setSigningOut(false);
    }
  };

  const displayName = user?.user_metadata?.full_name || user?.user_metadata?.name || user?.email || '';
  const initials = displayName
    ? displayName
        .split(' ')
        .map((part: string) => part[0])
        .filter(Boolean)
        .join('')
        .slice(0, 2)
        .toUpperCase()
    : 'JD';

  return (
    <header className="h-14 bg-[#1d2d3e] text-white px-5 flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#141f2b] shadow-sm shrink-0 z-20">
      {/* Left section: TestRail Section Breadcrumb */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-semibold tracking-wide">
          <span className="inline-block w-2 h-2 rounded-full bg-[#48a43f]"></span>
          <span className="text-white font-medium">Dashboard Overview</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Right section: Org Badge, Theme Toggle, CTA, User Menu */}
      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
        {organizationLoadState === 'available' ? (
          <div className="hidden sm:flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-medium text-slate-200 bg-[#2b3d51] px-3 py-1.5 rounded border border-[#384e66]">
            <Building2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-400" />
            <span>{organizationName}</span>
          </div>
        ) : organizationLoadState === 'empty' ? (
          <Link
            href="/organizations/new"
            className="hidden sm:inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium text-slate-300 hover:text-white bg-[#2b3d51] hover:bg-[#344961] px-3 py-1.5 rounded transition-colors"
          >
            <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-[#48a43f]" />
            Create organization
          </Link>
        ) : organizationLoadState === 'error' ? (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setOrganizationRetry((retry) => retry + 1)}
            className="text-xs text-amber-300 hover:text-amber-200 bg-amber-950/40 border border-amber-800/60 px-2.5 py-1 rounded"
            title="Retry loading your organization"
          >
            Organization unavailable · Retry
          </button>
        ) : null}

        {/* TestRail Signature Green "New Project" Button */}
        <Link
          href="/projects/new"
          className="bg-[#48a43f] hover:bg-[#3c8b34] text-white text-xs font-semibold px-3 py-1.5 rounded shadow-sm transition-colors flex items-center gap-1.5 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48a43f] focus:ring-offset-1 focus:ring-offset-[#1d2d3e]"
        >
          <Plus className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[2.5]" />
          <span>New Project</span>
        </Link>

        {/* Dark / Light Mode Switcher */}
        <ThemeToggle />

        {/* User Avatar & TestRail Style Dropdown */}
        <div className="relative">
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => { setMenuOpen((open) => !open); setSignOutError(''); }}
            className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#276fbf] hover:ring-2 hover:ring-[#48a43f] flex items-center justify-center text-white text-xs font-bold transition-all shadow-sm focus:outline-none"
            aria-label="Open account menu"
            aria-haspopup="menu"
            aria-expanded={menuOpen}
          >
            {initials || 'QA'}
          </button>

          {menuOpen && (
            <div
              role="menu"
              className="absolute right-0 top-10 z-50 w-64 rounded-md border border-[#d8dee4] dark:border-[#28374d] bg-white dark:bg-[#182232] p-1.5 shadow-xl text-slate-800 dark:text-slate-200"
            >
              <div className="px-3 py-2 border-b border-slate-100 dark:border-[#28374d] mb-1">
                <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-900 dark:text-white">{user?.user_metadata?.full_name || 'QA Engineer'}</p>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">{user?.email}</p>
                <span className="inline-block mt-1 text-[10px] font-semibold bg-[#e8f5e9] dark:bg-[#132c1c] text-[#2e7d32] dark:text-[#52c41a] border border-[#c8e6c9] dark:border-[#1d4c2b] px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full">
                  Organization Member
                </span>
              </div>
              <Link
                href="/profile"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
                className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-slate-700 dark:text-slate-300 hover:bg-slate-50 dark:hover:bg-[#202c3e] hover:text-slate-900 dark:hover:text-white transition-colors"
              >
                <User className="w-3.5 h-3.5 text-slate-500 dark:text-slate-400" />
                My Profile
              </Link>
              <div className="border-t border-slate-100 dark:border-[#28374d] my-1"></div>
              <button
                type="button"
                role="menuitem"
                onClick={handleSignOut}
                disabled={signingOut}
                className="w-full flex items-center gap-2 text-left rounded px-3 py-2 text-xs font-medium text-red-600 dark:text-red-400 hover:bg-red-50 dark:hover:bg-red-950/30 disabled:opacity-50 transition-colors"
              >
                <LogOut className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                {signingOut ? 'Signing out...' : 'Sign out'}
              </button>
              {signOutError && <p role="alert" className="px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-red-600 font-medium">{signOutError}</p>}
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </header>
  );
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\components\dashboard\stats-card.tsx =====
interface StatsCardProps {
  label: string;
  value: string;
  change?: string;
  trend?: 'up' | 'down';
}

export function StatsCard({ label, value, change, trend = 'up' }: StatsCardProps) {
  return (
    <div className="bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded-lg p-5 shadow-sm border-t-2 border-t-[#276fbf]">
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
        <div>
          <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-[#6e7d8c] uppercase tracking-wide">{label}</p>
          <p className="text-2xl font-bold text-[#1d2d3e] mt-1.5">{value}</p>
        </div>
        {change && (
          <span className={`text-xs font-bold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded ${trend === 'up' ? 'bg-green-50 text-[#3d8c36]' : 'bg-red-50 text-red-600'}`}>
            {change}
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export type ProjectOption = {
  id: string;
  organization_id: string;
  key: string;
  name: string;
};

export function ProjectSelector({
  value,
  onChange,
  disabled = false,
}: {
  value: string;
  onChange: (project: ProjectOption | null) => void;
  disabled?: boolean;
}) {
  const [projects, setProjects] = useState<ProjectOption[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');

  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;

    fetch('/api/projects', { cache: 'no-store' })
      .then(async (response) => {
        const data = await response.json();
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(data.error || 'Could not load projects.');
        return (data.projects ?? []) as ProjectOption[];
      })
      .then((items) => {
        if (!active) return;
        setProjects(items);
        if (items.length > 0) onChange(items.find((item) => item.id === value) ?? items[0]);
      })
      .catch((loadError: unknown) => {
        if (active) setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : 'Could not load projects.');
      })
      .finally(() => { if (active) setLoading(false); });

    return () => { active = false; };
  // This runs once so a parent state update does not refetch the same options.
  // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);

  if (loading) return <p className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">Loading projects...</p>;
  if (error) return <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-red-600">{error}</p>;
  if (projects.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className="text-xs text-[#6e7d8c]">
        No active projects found. <Link href="/projects/new" className="text-[#276fbf] hover:underline font-semibold">Create a project first</Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <select
      value={value}
      onChange={(event) => onChange(projects.find((project) => project.id === event.target.value) ?? null)}
      className="w-full px-3 py-2 bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] rounded text-sm text-[#1d2d3e] focus:outline-none focus:border-[#48a43f] focus:ring-1 focus:ring-[#48a43f] transition"
      required
      disabled={disabled}
    >
      <option value="" disabled>Select a project</option>
      {projects.map((project) => (
        <option key={project.id} value={project.id}>{project.key} — {project.name}</option>
      ))}
    </select>
  );
}

'use client';

import { useState } from 'react';

interface MemberInvitationProps {
  organizationId: string;
}

export function MemberInvitation({ organizationId }: MemberInvitationProps) {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [role, setRole] = useState('qa_engineer');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [message, setMessage] = useState('');

  const handleInvite = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setLoading(true);
    setMessage('');

    // Simulate invitation
    setTimeout(() => {
      setLoading(false);
      setMessage(`Invitation sent to ${email}`);
      setEmail('');
    }, 1000);
  };

  return (
    <div className="bg-secondary border border-border rounded-lg p-6">
      <h3 className="text-lg font-semibold text-foreground mb-4">Invite Team Member</h3>
      
      <form onSubmit={handleInvite} className="space-y-4">
        <div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-foreground mb-2">
            Email Address
          </label>
          <input
            type="email"
            value={email}
            onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-background border border-border rounded-md text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary"
            placeholder="colleague@company.com"
            required
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="block text-sm font-medium text-foreground mb-2">
            Role
          </label>
          <select
            value={role}
            onChange={(e) => setRole(e.target.value)}
            className="w-full px-4 py-2 bg-background border border-border rounded-md text-foreground focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-primary"
          >
            <option value="qa_engineer">QA Engineer</option>
            <option value="qa_manager">QA Manager</option>
            <option value="developer">Developer</option>
            <option value="viewer">Viewer</option>
          </select>
        </div>

        {message && (
          <div className="text-sm text-green-500">{message}</div>
        )}

        <button
          type="submit"
          disabled={loading}
          className="w-full py-2 bg-primary text-white rounded-md hover:bg-primary/90 disabled:opacity-50"
        >
          {loading ? 'Sending...' : 'Send Invitation'}
        </button>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}

===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\components\theme\ThemeProvider.tsx =====
'use client';

import React, { createContext, useContext, useEffect, useState } from 'react';

type Theme = 'light' | 'dark';

interface ThemeContextType {
  theme: Theme;
  toggleTheme: () => void;
  setTheme: (theme: Theme) => void;
}

const ThemeContext = createContext<ThemeContextType>({
  theme: 'light',
  toggleTheme: () => {},
  setTheme: () => {},
});

export function ThemeProvider({ children }: { children: React.ReactNode }) {
  const [theme, setThemeState] = useState<Theme>('light');
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Read from localStorage or system preference
    const stored = localStorage.getItem('qa_theme') as Theme | null;
    if (stored === 'dark' || stored === 'light') {
      setThemeState(stored);
      if (stored === 'dark') {
        document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
      } else {
        document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
      }
    } else if (window.matchMedia && window.matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches) {
      setThemeState('dark');
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
    }
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  const setTheme = (newTheme: Theme) => {
    setThemeState(newTheme);
    localStorage.setItem('qa_theme', newTheme);
    if (newTheme === 'dark') {
      document.documentElement.classList.add('dark');
    } else {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('dark');
    }
  };

  const toggleTheme = () => {
    setTheme(theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark');
  };

  return (
    <ThemeContext.Provider value={{ theme, toggleTheme, setTheme }}>
      {children}
    </ThemeContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useTheme() {
  return useContext(ThemeContext);
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\components\theme\ThemeToggle.tsx =====
'use client';

import { useTheme } from './ThemeProvider';
import { Sun, Moon } from 'lucide-react';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';

export function ThemeToggle({ className = '' }: { className?: string }) {
  const { theme, toggleTheme } = useTheme();
  const [mounted, setMounted] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setMounted(true);
  }, []);

  if (!mounted) {
    return (
      <div className={`w-8 h-8 rounded flex items-center justify-center bg-white/10 ${className}`}>
        <div className="w-4 h-4 rounded-full bg-white/20 animate-pulse" />
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <button
      onClick={toggleTheme}
      type="button"
      title={`Switch to ${theme === 'dark' ? 'Light' : 'Dark'} mode`}
      aria-label={`Switch to ${theme === 'dark' ? 'Light' : 'Dark'} mode`}
      className={`w-8 h-8 rounded flex items-center justify-center text-slate-200 hover:text-white hover:bg-white/10 transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-white/30 ${className}`}
    >
      {theme === 'dark' ? (
        <Sun className="w-4 h-4 text-amber-400 transition-transform hover:rotate-45" />
      ) : (
        <Moon className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-300 transition-transform hover:-rotate-12" />
      )}
    </button>
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\components\ui\button.tsx =====
import { cn } from '@/lib/utils';

interface ButtonProps extends React.ButtonHTMLAttributes<HTMLButtonElement> {
  variant?: 'primary' | 'secondary' | 'outline' | 'danger';
  size?: 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg';
}

export function Button({ 
  className, 
  variant = 'primary', 
  size = 'md',
  ...props 
}: ButtonProps) {
  const baseStyles = 'inline-flex items-center justify-center rounded font-semibold transition-colors focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#48a43f] focus:ring-offset-1 disabled:opacity-50 disabled:pointer-events-none shadow-sm';
  
  const variants = {
    primary: 'bg-[#48a43f] text-white hover:bg-[#3d8c36] active:bg-[#32722b]',
    secondary: 'bg-white border border-[#d8dee4] text-[#4f6174] hover:bg-[#f5f7f9] hover:text-[#1d2d3e]',
    outline: 'bg-transparent border border-[#d8dee4] text-[#4f6174] hover:bg-[#f5f7f9]',
    danger: 'bg-red-600 text-white hover:bg-red-700 active:bg-red-800',
  };
  
  const sizes = {
    sm: 'h-8 px-3 text-xs',
    md: 'h-9 px-4 text-xs',
    lg: 'h-11 px-5 text-sm',
  };
  
  return (
    <button
      className={cn(baseStyles, variants[variant], sizes[size], className)}
      {...props}
    />
  );
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\lib\utils.ts =====
// Shared Utility Functions

export function generateId(prefix: string): string {
  const timestamp = Date.now().toString(36);
  const random = Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8);
  return `${prefix}-${timestamp}-${random}`.toUpperCase();
}

export function formatDate(date: string | Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
  }).format(new Date(date));
}

export function formatDateTime(date: string | Date): string {
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
    year: 'numeric',
    month: 'short',
    day: 'numeric',
    hour: '2-digit',
    minute: '2-digit',
  }).format(new Date(date));
}

export function cn(...classes: (string | boolean | undefined | null)[]): string {
  return classes.filter(Boolean).join(' ');
}

export function truncateText(text: string, maxLength: number): string {
  if (text.length <= maxLength) return text;
  return text.substring(0, maxLength) + '...';
}

export function getStatusColor(status: string): string {
  const colors: Record<string, string> = {
    draft: 'bg-gray-500',
    under_review: 'bg-yellow-500',
    approved: 'bg-green-500',
    implemented: 'bg-blue-500',
    verified: 'bg-emerald-500',
    rejected: 'bg-red-500',
    new: 'bg-blue-500',
    open: 'bg-orange-500',
    in_progress: 'bg-purple-500',
    fixed: 'bg-green-500',
    ready_for_retest: 'bg-yellow-500',
    closed: 'bg-gray-500',
    passed: 'bg-green-500',
    failed: 'bg-red-500',
    blocked: 'bg-orange-500',
    not_run: 'bg-gray-500',
    not_applicable: 'bg-gray-400',
  };
  return colors[status] || 'bg-gray-500';
}

export function calculateProgress(completed: number, total: number): number {
  if (total === 0) return 0;
  return Math.round((completed / total) * 100);
}

export function getPriorityColor(priority: string): string {
  const colors: Record<string, string> = {
    low: 'bg-green-500',
    medium: 'bg-yellow-500',
    high: 'bg-orange-500',
    critical: 'bg-red-500',
  };
  return colors[priority] || 'bg-gray-500';
}

export function getPermissions(role: UserRole): UserPermissions {
  return PERMISSIONS[role];
}

export function hasPermission(role: UserRole, permission: keyof UserPermissions): boolean {
  return PERMISSIONS[role][permission] === true;
}

export function canAccessModule(role: UserRole, module: 'projects' | 'requirements' | 'test-cases' | 'test-plans' | 'test-execution' | 'defects' | 'reports' | 'ai-assistant' | 'settings'): boolean {
  const perms = PERMISSIONS[role];

  switch (module) {
    case 'projects':
      return perms.canCreateProject || perms.canEditProject || perms.canViewReports;
    case 'requirements':
      return perms.canCreateRequirement || perms.canEditRequirement || perms.canViewReports;
    case 'test-cases':
      return perms.canCreateTestCase || perms.canEditTestCase || perms.canViewReports;
    case 'test-plans':
      return perms.canCreateTestPlan || perms.canEditTestPlan || perms.canViewReports;
    case 'test-execution':
      return perms.canExecuteTests || perms.canViewReports;
    case 'defects':
      return perms.canCreateDefect || perms.canEditDefect || perms.canViewReports;
    case 'reports':
      return perms.canViewReports;
    case 'ai-assistant':
      return perms.role !== 'viewer';
    case 'settings':
      return perms.canManageOrganization || perms.canInviteMembers;
    default:
      return false;
  }
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\lib\auth\rbac.ts =====
import { getCurrentUserWithOrg } from './session';
import { canAccessModule, hasPermission } from './permissions';
import type { UserRole } from '@/types/supabase';

export async function checkPermission(permission: string) {
  const userData = await getCurrentUserWithOrg();
  
  if (!userData?.user) {
    return { allowed: false, user: null };
  }

  const role = userData.currentOrg?.role as UserRole;
  
  if (!role) {
    return { allowed: false, user: userData.user, role: null };
  }

  const allowed = hasPermission(role, permission as any);
  
  return { allowed, user: userData.user, role, permissions: userData };
}

export async function requirePermission(permission: string) {
  const check = await checkPermission(permission);
  
  if (!check.allowed) {
    throw new Error('Insufficient permissions');
  }
  
  return check;
}

export async function checkModuleAccess(module: string) {
  const userData = await getCurrentUserWithOrg();
  
  if (!userData?.user) {
    return false;
  }

  const role = userData.currentOrg?.role as UserRole;
  
  if (!role) {
    return false;
  }

  return canAccessModule(role, module as any);
}



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\lib\auth\session.ts =====
import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';
import { Database } from '@/types/supabase';

export async function getCurrentUser() {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();
  return user;
}

export async function getCurrentUserWithOrg() {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data: { user } } = await supabase.auth.getUser();

  if (!user) return null;

  // Get user organizations
  const { data: memberships } = await supabase
    .from('organization_members')
    .select(`
      role,
      organization_id,
      organizations!inner(id, name, slug)
    `)
    .eq('user_id', user.id);

  return {
    user,
    organizations: memberships || [],
    currentOrg: memberships?.[0],
  };
}

export async function getUserOrganizationIds(userId: string) {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  const supabase = createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );

  const { data } = await supabase
    .from('organization_members')
    .select('organization_id')
    .eq('user_id', userId);

  return data?.map(m => m.organization_id) || [];
}

// Supabase Client Initialization
// Supports both browser and server contexts for Next.js

import { createBrowserClient } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { createClient } from '@supabase/supabase-js';
import type { Database } from '@/types/supabase';

const supabaseUrl = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL || 'https://placeholder.supabase.co';
const supabaseAnonKey = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY || 'placeholder-key';

// Browser client (client components) - automatically manages cookies for SSR and middleware
export const supabase = createBrowserClient<Database>(supabaseUrl, supabaseAnonKey);

// Server client (server components / route handlers)
export const createServerSupabaseClient = () => {
  return createClient<Database>(
    supabaseUrl,
    process.env.SUPABASE_SECRET_KEY || process.env.SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY || 'placeholder-key',
    {
      auth: {
        persistSession: false,
      },
    }
  );
};



===== FILE: C:\Users\engmi\.claude\qa-management-platform\src\lib\supabase\rls-policies.sql =====
-- ============================================
-- ROW LEVEL SECURITY (RLS) POLICIES
-- Multi-Tenant Organization Isolation
-- ============================================

-- Enable RLS on all tables
ALTER TABLE organizations ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE profiles ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE organization_members ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE projects ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE releases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE requirements ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_cases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_case_steps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_case_requirements ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_plans ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_suites ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_suite_cases ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_runs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE test_executions ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE execution_steps ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE defects ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

-- Helper function to get current user's organization IDs
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION get_user_organization_ids(user_uuid UUID)
RETURNS SETOF UUID AS $$
BEGIN
  RETURN QUERY
  SELECT organization_id FROM organization_members WHERE user_id = user_uuid;
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Helper function to check if user is org admin
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION is_org_admin(user_uuid UUID, org_id UUID)
RETURNS BOOLEAN AS $$
BEGIN
  RETURN EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM organization_members 
    WHERE user_id = user_uuid 
    AND organization_id = org_id 
    AND role IN ('super_admin', 'org_admin')
  );
END;
$$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;

-- Organizations Policy
CREATE POLICY org_isolation_policy ON organizations
  FOR ALL USING (id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Organization Members Policy
CREATE POLICY org_members_isolation ON organization_members
  FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Projects Policy
CREATE POLICY projects_isolation ON projects
  FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Projects Policy - Admin Only for Delete
CREATE POLICY projects_admin_delete ON projects
  FOR DELETE USING (
    organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid()))
    AND is_org_admin(auth.uid(), organization_id)
  );

-- Requirements Policy
CREATE POLICY requirements_isolation ON requirements
  FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Test Cases Policy
CREATE POLICY test_cases_isolation ON test_cases
  FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Test Case Steps Policy - Cascade from test_cases
CREATE POLICY test_case_steps_isolation ON test_case_steps
  FOR ALL USING (
    test_case_id IN (
      SELECT id FROM test_cases 
      WHERE organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid()))
    )
  );

-- Test Case Requirements Policy - both related records must be visible to the user.
CREATE POLICY test_case_requirements_isolation ON test_case_requirements
  FOR ALL USING (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM requirements r
      JOIN test_cases tc ON tc.project_id = r.project_id
      WHERE r.id = test_case_requirements.requirement_id
        AND tc.id = test_case_requirements.test_case_id
    )
  )
  WITH CHECK (
    EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM requirements r
      JOIN test_cases tc ON tc.project_id = r.project_id
      WHERE r.id = test_case_requirements.requirement_id
        AND tc.id = test_case_requirements.test_case_id
    )
  );

-- Test Plans Policy
CREATE POLICY test_plans_isolation ON test_plans
  FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Test Suites Policy - Cascade from test_plans
CREATE POLICY test_suites_isolation ON test_suites
  FOR ALL USING (
    test_plan_id IN (
      SELECT id FROM test_plans 
      WHERE organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid()))
    )
  );

-- Test Runs Policy
CREATE POLICY test_runs_isolation ON test_runs
  FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Test Executions Policy
CREATE POLICY test_executions_isolation ON test_executions
  FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

-- Execution Steps Policy - Cascade from test_executions
CREATE POLICY execution_steps_isolation ON execution_steps
  FOR ALL USING (
    test_execution_id IN (
      SELECT id FROM test_executions 
      WHERE organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid()))
    )
  );

-- Defects Policy
CREATE POLICY defects_isolation ON defects
  FOR ALL USING (organization_id IN (SELECT get_user_organization_ids(auth.uid())));

import { createServerClient, type CookieOptions } from '@supabase/ssr';
import { cookies } from 'next/headers';

export function createRouteSupabaseClient() {
  const cookieStore = cookies();
  return createServerClient(
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_URL!,
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY!,
    {
      cookies: {
        get(name: string) {
          return cookieStore.get(name)?.value;
        },
        set(name: string, value: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value, ...options });
        },
        remove(name: string, options: CookieOptions) {
          cookieStore.set({ name, value: '', ...options });
        },
      },
    }
  );
}

export type UserRole = 'super_admin' | 'org_admin' | 'qa_manager' | 'qa_engineer' | 'developer' | 'viewer';
export type TestStatus = 'not_run' | 'passed' | 'failed' | 'blocked' | 'not_applicable';
export type DefectStatus = 'new' | 'open' | 'in_progress' | 'fixed' | 'ready_for_retest' | 'reopened' | 'closed' | 'rejected';
export type RequirementStatus = 'draft' | 'under_review' | 'approved' | 'implemented' | 'verified' | 'rejected';
